## Selection, projection and capability

One `ColdSelection` has `tenant_id,site_id,code,from_at,to_at,known_at,watermark,foundation_revision_ids,binding_revision_ids,assessment_revision_id,finding_revision_ids,source_gate`. `from_at` and `to_at` are UTC and half-open; `known_at` defaults to latest committed disposition watermark captured once at request start. Omitted `from,to` selects W2-03's latest settled cold window; exactly one omitted is 422. The effective Foundation/binding is selected at each evidence source time (W2-03), with the selected policy at the requested window; conflicting/absent effective revision is an explicit limitation. 31 days maximum. No newer assessment, Finding or consequence may be silently mixed with an older `known_at`. A late accepted report makes the current projection `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until a new exact assessment and cold consequence both settle. A selected old cutoff is `HISTORICAL`, even when there is no pending work. Determine probe freshness from the latest qualifying accepted probe `source_time` within `[from,to)` compared with `to`: over 10 minutes is `STALE_SOURCE`. Later wall-clock viewing of the 2026-10-06 replay never alone makes it stale or live. Precedence: overlapping queued/failed newer work -> `PENDING_RECOMPUTE`; explicit cutoff older than latest relevant committed disposition -> `HISTORICAL`; no settled selection/probe or future `to` -> `UNKNOWN`; old last probe -> `STALE_SOURCE`; otherwise `CURRENT`. Money can remain `UNPRICED` within a current technical read; pending/old/unresolved money has its own withholding limit.

`ColdSiteReadService` joins, by exact UUIDs, W2-03 `ColdReadService` output, W2-01 Foundation, S4-05 source/publication links and the cold consequence head. It does not use a display-name join or the latest Finding irrespective of window. Capability claims are exactly `TEMPERATURE_POINT,EXCURSION_BOUNDS,BACKUP_START_RESULT,TRANSFER_POSITION,COOLING_RESPONSE,PRODUCT_EXPOSURE`. Each has `state:SUPPORTED|LIMITED|UNAVAILABLE|CONTRADICTORY`, `required_roles:string[]`, `missing_roles:string[]`, `coverage:{known_points:int,expected_points:int,ratio:decimal string}` and `as_of,limitation`. `TEMPERATURE_POINT` requires GOOD calibrated room probe and attested clock; `EXCURSION_BOUNDS` adds policy, two qualifying samples and >=0.8 coverage with no >10-minute bridge; `BACKUP_START_RESULT` requires correlated accepted request/result and the W2-03 causal rules; `TRANSFER_POSITION` needs its own bound transfer report; `COOLING_RESPONSE` needs an accepted compressor state and probe series; `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` means bounded thermal exposure only and requires an eligible excursion. Contradictory accepted facts give `CONTRADICTORY`, not healthy/failure. Source `contact` derives from a diagnostic received no later than selected `known_at` only when that diagnostic actually identifies the source; if only gateway-level contact exists, source contact is `UNKNOWN` with a limitation. `fact_age` is a nonnegative integer number of seconds/null between selected `to` and the last accepted source fact in `[from,to)` at the cutoff; it is distinct from contact. No source contact becomes equipment condition. Panel 3's â€œnext evidenceâ€ is simply a named missing role/link, never a scored recommendation.

Analysis rows are selected accepted public records from the pinned assessment basis and exact links, ordered `(source_time,evidence_id)`, with `evidence_id,kind,source_time,received_at,quality,role,exclusion,source_id,envelope_sha256,assessment_revision_id`; `exclusion` is W2-03's SUPPORT/CONTEXT/EXCLUDED reason or null. Keep a record in the list when excluded, label why, and link to the existing Site Evidence exact record when source gate permits. `claim_rules` lists rule ID `cold-excursion/1` or `cold-start-response/1`, requirement, observed/missing role, coverage and resulting limit, all derived from the selected assessment; no speculative prose rule. Opaque cursor encodes `(source_time,evidence_id)` and selected basis SHA; mismatch returns 409 rather than changing pages.

## Money schema `0023` and rules

Create four new tables (do not widen the mini-grid `financial_consequence_revision`, whose CHECK constrains `POTENTIAL_FUEL_COST`, `fuel-cost-1`, L and priced amount). The Site FK uses `(tenant_id,site_id)` -> `site(tenant_id,id)`; references to FindingRevision, cold episode revision, assessment revision and accepted evidence use composite `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` keys; the source link uses `(tenant_id,site_id,batch_id)`. Add scoped unique targets to referenced tables only where `0022` has not already supplied them. All tables have `tenant_id,site_id`; every immutable row has server `created_at`/`finished_at`, with UPDATE/DELETE refusal triggers. Downgrade refuses while any of these rows exist; otherwise drops only W2-04 triggers/tables/indexes, including its added scoped unique target on the older link table.

* `cold_consequence_revision`: `id uuid PK`, `finding_revision_id`, `episode_revision_id`, `assessment_revision_id` UUID NOT NULL; `revision_no int >0`, `prior_revision_id uuid NULL`; `kind` CHECK `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE|RECORDED_REPAIR`; `state` CHECK `UNPRICED|WITHHELD`; `reason` nonempty; `window_start,window_end timestamptz` with start<end; `room_asset_id uuid`; `overlap_group text`; `quantity_unit` CHECK `degC_min|none`; `quantity_low,quantity_high numeric NULL` nonnegative/ordered; `amount_low,amount_high numeric NULL`, `currency char(3) NULL`; `finding_basis_sha256,consequence_basis_sha256 char(64)`, `model_id` CHECK `cold-exposure/1|cold-repair/1`; `source_classification`, `source_gate_sha256 char(64)`, `assumption_revision_ids jsonb` array, `limitation text NULL`. Unique `(finding_revision_id,kind,revision_no)`, `(finding_revision_id,kind,consequence_basis_sha256)` and scoped `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` for evidence links. DB CHECK requires `amount_low IS NULL AND amount_high IS NULL AND currency IS NULL` for every row; a later approved migration must widen this. `quantity_unit='none'` requires both quantity bounds null; `degC_min` allows null bounds only when the technical claim is limited/withheld. No `RECORDED_REPAIR` row is created for the six replays because no exact accepted cost/work feed exists. `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` may carry nonnegative bounded `degC_min`; if insufficient evidence both bounds null. No zero amount or stock count stands for unknown.
* `cold_consequence_evidence`: `consequence_revision_id,evidence_id,use,reason` with PK `(consequence_revision_id,evidence_id,use)`, `use=SUPPORT|CONTEXT|EXCLUDED` and scoped FKs. Include all exact evidence cited by the pinned Finding/episode basis, no private world keys.
* `cold_consequence_work`: `id uuid PK`, `cause` CHECK `FINDING_REVISION_APPENDED|EPISODE_REVISION_APPENDED|SOURCE_GATE_CHANGED`; nullable `finding_revision_id`, `episode_revision_id`, `publication_batch_id`, with a CHECK requiring exactly the locator for its cause, and scoped FKs; `window_start,window_end` (nonempty, half-open), `room_asset_id`, `dirty_key_sha256 char(64)`, `enqueued_at`; unique `(tenant_id,site_id,dirty_key_sha256)` and `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` for attempts. Add `(tenant_id,site_id,batch_id)` uniqueness to `batch_publication_link` in 0023 for the scoped `publication_batch_id` FK; `batch_id` is that table's actual PK, there is no link `id`. Trigger-only INSERT with `ON CONFLICT (tenant_id,site_id,dirty_key_sha256) DO NOTHING`. A cold FindingRevision or episode revision enqueues its selected Site/window/room; the source-gate trigger enqueues only overlapping heads. No runtime work insert/status update. Backfill existing W2-03 cold heads once during upgrade, idempotently. `0010_financial_assumptions` has a diesel-only DB CHECK as well as a diesel-only publisher: **no `ASSUMPTION_PUBLISHED` cause or trigger in 0023**. A future cold assumption contract must migrate the DB, publisher and dirty-work cause together.
* `cold_consequence_work_attempt`: `id uuid PK`, `work_id` scoped FK, `attempt_no int >0`, `outcome` CHECK `SUCCEEDED|FAILED`, `error_code text NULL`, `consequence_revision_ids jsonb` array, `finished_at`; unique `(work_id,attempt_no)`. Success has no error and exact returned IDs; failure has error and empty IDs. A failed attempt leaves work pending; pending means no successful attempt. Index `(site_id,enqueued_at)` and `(work_id,attempt_no DESC)`.

`overlap_group = 'cold:' + sha256(canonical JSON array [tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,room_asset_id])`: literal lowercase `cold:` prefix, then 64 lowercase hex characters; it is a grouping label, not a V4 event/correlation ID (whose grammar excludes `:`). An excursion and backup Finding of the same selected room/window share it; the financial read groups `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` by this key and takes the single selected excursion exposure basis, never sums both. The start-response Finding may carry a `WITHHELD` context consequence with the same key but zero *counted* exposure entries, not a numeric zero. No cross-window summing without an explicit disjoint-window contract.

The source-gate event is an appended `batch_publication_link` to a previously accepted cold batch, if one exists; its trigger selects only cold heads whose linked accepted IDs use that batch and enqueues `SOURCE_GATE_CHANGED` inside the link transaction. An initial link before acceptance has no heads and creates no money work; later W2-03 Finding/episode append enqueues it. `evidence_publication` is immutable and a new unrelated publication never dirties an existing cold basis. Each trigger derives the same canonical dirty key from `(cause,locator,tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,room_asset_id)` and takes a Site advisory transaction lock before selecting heads, following `0012_evidence_consequence_work.py`'s enqueue lock pattern. The FindingRevision trigger is restricted to `NEW.domain='cold_chain'`. An episode with no warranted Finding still enqueues; the worker records a successful no-op attempt with an empty consequence ID array. No trigger on an accepted disposition bypasses W2-03 assessment: it waits for the new exact cold Finding/episode revision.

`cold-exposure/1` derives observed rectangle degree-minute bounds only from W2-03's persisted episode bounds/accepted evidence, not a continuous curve. A bounded positive or zero technical quantity is allowed only when W2-03 actually proved it; missing-probe/contradictory has null quantity. `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` is unpriced for sound technical bounds because no `cold_stock_quantity`, `cold_stock_unit_value`, reviewed `cold_exposure_rule`, product/currency binding or source-gated valuation exists. These are **reserved future scenario keys**, displayed as missing, not publishable via the diesel-price publisher. Product value/currency and EV tariff would be explicitly authored scenario assumptions and still would not prove sales, collections, spoilage or lost revenue. Recorded repair needs an accepted exact cost/currency/work record; none of the six replay cases has one. Mixed/unknown source, closed S4-05 gate, stale/pending assessment, incomplete period or contradictory evidence yields `WITHHELD` and null amount. LR current-money gate also refuses a current money claim from historical/pending basis. `exact_basis` returns selected Finding/episode/assessment UUIDs, accepted evidence UUIDs, source gate digest, assumption revision UUIDs (empty in W2) and consequence basis SHA. Basis SHA hashes canonical sorted IDs, window/cutoff, model/formula version, quantity bounds, source gate and missing assumption markers; an identical basis returns the existing ID and a changed basis appends a new revision.

`PostgresConsequenceStore` alone inserts `cold_consequence_revision`, `cold_consequence_evidence` and `cold_consequence_work_attempt`; add separate cold methods without changing the existing mini-grid methods. `cold_recompute.process_one` selects unfinished work under `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('cold_consequence:' || work_id,0))`, takes one repeatable-read snapshot, refuses a foreign/missing exact basis, writes consequence/links and `SUCCEEDED` attempt in one transaction. On derivation failure it rolls back that transaction and appends a typed `FAILED` attempt in a fresh transaction. A crash before success commits leaves no partial revision; retries are idempotent. `run` uses the existing `financials/recompute.py:retry_due_at` 1,2,4,8,16-minute schedule and bounded batch; one bad work item cannot starve others. Trigger-created work does not depend on a successful worker.

## Five API reads

`cold_site_api.py:build_cold_site_router` mounts five GET paths below `/api/sites/{site_code}/cold`. Request query for all is optional paired `from,to`, optional `known_at` (strict UTC); Analysis additionally `cursor,limit` (default 50, max 200), matching W2-03's paging spelling. Use W2-03 shared common envelope exactly: `site_id,window:{from,to,known_at},currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since},source_provenance:{classification,source_ids,evidence_ids,basis_sha256},limitations`. Every card/object includes `as_of` and `limitation`; scalar decimals serialize as decimal strings and absent values as JSON null. `currentness.state` is `CURRENT|PENDING_RECOMPUTE|STALE_SOURCE|HISTORICAL|UNKNOWN`. Valid empty selection is 200 with `UNKNOWN` and empty arrays. Use one selected repeatable-read snapshot for W2-03, source disclosure and consequence heads. No previous consequence becomes a current money claim while a relevant cold work item is queued or failed. Errors use `{error:{code,message}}`; never downgrade 503 into a blank page.

Refusal code contract (same scope and parse semantics as `cold_assessment_api.py:build_cold_assessment_router`): 401 `UNAUTHENTICATED`; 403 `SITE_SCOPE_FORBIDDEN` or `COLD_SOURCE_DISCLOSURE_FORBIDDEN`; 404 `SITE_NOT_FOUND`; 409 `COLD_SELECTION_UNSETTLED` for an explicitly requested revision that has no settled exact basis or `COLD_CURSOR_BASIS_CHANGED` for a changed Analysis basis; 422 `COLD_TIME_INVALID`, `COLD_WINDOW_INVALID`, `COLD_WINDOW_TOO_LONG`, `COLD_PARAMETER_UNKNOWN`, `COLD_LIMIT_INVALID`, or `COLD_CURSOR_INVALID`; 503 `COLD_READ_UNAVAILABLE`. A wrong-type/foreign Site is 404 after access is checked. An absent optional current basis is 200 `UNKNOWN`, not a 409. Analysis cursor is unpadded base64url of sorted, compact UTF-8 JSON `{basis:<64 lowercase hex>,id:<UUID string>,t:<UTC instant>}`; malformed is 422 and an exact basis mismatch is 409. No arbitrary `after` offset or page across a changed basis.

| GET suffix | Exact additional response |
| --- | --- |
| `/overview` | `status:{state:OBSERVED_EXCURSION|NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,claim_level,as_of,limitation}`, `objective:{text,policy_low_deg_c,policy_high_deg_c,room_asset_id,as_of,limitation}`, `temperature_summary:{latest_deg_c,latest_at,coverage:{known_points,expected_points,ratio},episode_count,claim_level,as_of,limitation}`, `backup_summary:{attempts,observed_success,observed_failure,unknown,latest_chain_id,as_of,limitation}`, `causal_strip:[{step:GRID|START_REQUEST|START_RESULT|GENERATOR|TRANSFER|BUS|COMPRESSOR|PROBE,state:OBSERVED|MISSING|CONTRADICTORY,at,evidence_id,as_of,limitation}]`, `findings:{count,items:[{finding_id,revision_id,title,claim_level,link}],as_of,limitation}`, `needs_attention:[{code,text,link,as_of,limitation}]`, `consequence_summary:{state:UNPRICED|WITHHELD|UNKNOWN,quantity:{low,high,unit:'degC_min'|null},amount:{low:null,high:null,currency:null},overlap_group,as_of,limitation}`, `links:{temperature,backup,findings,evidence,analysis,financials}`. The response includes W2-03 selected temperature points only through its linked read, never repackages model truth. |
| `/evidence` | `capabilities:[{claim,state,required_roles,missing_roles,coverage,as_of,limitation}]`, `sources:[{id,role,contact,fact_age,accepted_count,last_accepted_at,as_of,limitation}]`, `links:{analysis,foundation,temperature,backup}`. `contact` is `RECENT|STALE|NEVER|UNKNOWN`; `fact_age` is duration/null, never equipment condition. |
| `/evidence/analysis` | `accepted_records:{items:[{evidence_id,kind,source_time,received_at,quality,role,exclusion,source_id,envelope_sha256,assessment_revision_id,link,as_of,limitation}],next_cursor}`, `claim_rules:[{rule_id,requirement,observed_roles,missing_roles,coverage,result,as_of,limitation}]`, `links:{evidence,findings}`. No private oracle or rejected ordinal masquerades as accepted. |
| `/financials` | `consequences:[{id,kind,state,quantity_bounds:{low,high},quantity_unit,amount_bounds:{low:null,high:null},currency:null,reason,overlap_group,exact_basis,as_of,limitation}]`, `not_included:[{code:STOCK_VALUE_UNDECLARED|EXPOSURE_RULE_UNAPPROVED|SPOILAGE_UNPROVED|REPAIR_COST_ABSENT|SOURCE_GATE_WITHHELD,text,as_of,limitation}]`, `links:{assumptions,findings,evidence}`. Return one counted exposure per overlap group; the optional backup context row is marked nonadditive. |
| `/financial-assumptions` | `items:[{key,revision_id,value,unit,currency,valid_from,valid_to,recorded_at,source,as_of,limitation}]`, `missing_keys:['cold_stock_quantity','cold_stock_unit_value','cold_exposure_rule']` in this slice, `publication_state:'NOT_SUPPORTED_IN_W2'`, `links:{financials}`. `items=[]` for six canonical replays. This is a read-only explicit absence statement, not a publication form. |

Links carry the exact selected `from,to,known_at` and record/revision ID through route builders, URL encoded. The Site overview's Finding count counts distinct current Finding IDs, not episode revisions. `needs_attention` is ordered: pending recompute, probe gap, observed start failure, unknown transfer, missing policy; omit a code only when its predicate is false. No â€œnext actionâ€ link unless an actual W2-03 Finding or Evidence destination exists.

## Final Architect rulings on commit 1 (Q1-Q10)

This section overrides shorthand above where the two differ. Migration `0023` on `task/W2-04-cold-site-evidence-money` is provisional until its listed corrections are made; passing migration tests do not waive them.

1. **Q1 CHANGE â€” selected window and room.** Keep `assessment_revision.window_start/window_end` for each trigger; neither episode bounds nor `window_assessment_revision_id` is a money window. For an `EXCURSION`, `cold_episode.asset_id` is the room. For a `START_RESPONSE`, that asset and the start Finding's `subject_asset_id` are the **generator**, so they must never fill `room_asset_id` or the overlap key. Resolve the effective chamber room from the exact assessment's selected `cold-roles-1` Foundation/binding basis (the same room that its temperature policy names); require one scoped room, or raise and roll back. Apply this to the Finding trigger, episode trigger, source-gate trigger and backfill. Prove that excursion and start Finding for one assessment produce the same room/window/group and that no generator ID is stored as a room.
2. **Q2 ACCEPT.** Source-gate link dirties latest cold episode heads citing the batch through `cold_episode_evidence` and latest cold Finding heads citing it through `finding_revision_evidence`. Scope every subquery by tenant and Site as well as parent ID; distinct heads may produce distinct work rows but identical selected consequence basis returns the same revision.
3. **Q3 CHANGE â€” preserve instant precision.** Canonical JSON array of strings and lowercase hyphenated UUIDs stand. Use UTC `Z` timestamps with six fractional digits **when microseconds are nonzero**, and whole seconds otherwise. This selected-window key is distinct from `source_envelope.instant_text`, which emits whole seconds for envelope publication; W2-03's `_instant` accepts fractional selected bounds. Reject precision beyond microseconds at the API boundary; never truncate a selected window to seconds when hashing. The six replay windows happen to have whole seconds. SQL and Python golden vectors must match for whole-second and fractional inputs.
4. **Q4 corrected against W2-03 data.** `limitation IS NOT NULL` means a named technical limit, not an arbitrary note. W2-03 can persist both observed rectangle degree-minute bounds while `claim.open=true`, `duration_high=NULL` and both end bounds are null. For every selected open excursion with a proved lower bound, store `quantity_low=episode.degree_minutes_low`, `quantity_high=NULL`, `state=UNPRICED` when no other withholding gate applies, and include `OPEN_EPISODE_UPPER_UNKNOWN`. The W2-03 finite observed-segment high must not become a high for total episode exposure. Apply this based on open/end state, not `episode.degree_minutes_high IS NULL`; the canonical excursion replay must carry the limit. For a closed, eligible excursion copy both proved bounds. `WITHHELD` may have either/both null bounds. Null amount/currency remains mandatory in every case.
5. **Q5 ACCEPT each extra.** Keep scoped self-FK, first-revision/prior-link CHECK, kind/model pairing, nonnull scoped room FK on revision and work, and nullable evidence-link reason. Add a same-Finding/kind predecessor check in store logic; FK alone does not prove chain membership. A successful no-Finding episode gets a no-op attempt, not a fabricated Finding or consequence.
6. **Q6 ACCEPT.** The listed `DEFAULT clock_timestamp()` columns, including evidence-link `created_at`, are server times. The sole store writer must omit them, and tests must assert they lie in the DB transaction's observed interval. Do not accept client timestamps in store methods. A forcing trigger is unnecessary for this owner-restricted append-only table.
7. **Q7 CHANGE â€” constrain the aggregate.** `source_classification` is exactly `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION|REGISTERED_SIMULATION|MIXED|UNKNOWN`, the values returned by `evidence/source_provenance.py`; add a SQL CHECK. `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` is not a current class. Preserve reason/detail and exact IDs separately; `UNKNOWN` and `MIXED` withhold money.
8. **Q8 ACCEPT.** Missing room or invalid assessment window is an invariant violation: raise and roll back the W2-03 assessment/Finding transaction, following `0012` rather than silently dropping dirty work. The worker reports `FAILED` for later derivation faults; it cannot repair a missing enqueue.
9. **Q9 ACCEPT with a nonvacuous guard.** Rule 8 scans runtime modules, so inspect the exact `0023` migration separately. Require at least one `INSERT INTO cold_consequence_work`, and require every such INSERT to use `ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, site_id, dirty_key_sha256) DO NOTHING`; reject `DO UPDATE`, another arbiter or a missing INSERT. Probe by mutating a temporary copy of the migration and restoring it, with pass and fail cases; no probe may pass solely because migrations are skipped by the runtime scan.
10. **Q10 ACCEPT staged enforcement.** Commit 1 installs table/owner/probe-name registration and refuses foreign and mutating writes. Commit 2 adds real store INSERTs and enables per-table `inserts seen` assertions for revision, evidence and attempt. Before commit 2 is declared done, all three must be nonvacuously seen; no empty/fake SQL is allowed to satisfy the guard. The trigger-only work check is active in commit 1.

## Commit-2 Architect rulings: stored codes and screen copy

These exact codes are approved with the meanings below. They are stable machine values, not user-facing text. The selected `/financials` response and overview money summary render the stated plain-language wording. Translate each code in the semicolon-joined stored `limitation` before display; never show that raw string to users. A limitation is independent of `state`: an open episode can be `UNPRICED` with an unknown upper bound. When multiple limits apply, present each distinct applicable sentence in a stable order; a valuation reason must not hide a technical or source limit.

| Code | Ruling and exact predicate | Plain screen wording |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `COLD_VALUATION_NOT_APPROVED` (reason) | Confirm for an eligible `UNPRICED` exposure; no approved cold stock value, currency or exposure valuation rule. This remains the reason for an open excursion with a proved lower bound. | `Thermal exposure is recorded, but no approved product valuation is available.` |
| `SOURCE_GATE_WITHHELD` (reason) | Confirm for `WITHHELD` when the exact source disclosure/provenance gate is closed, mixed or unknown. Do not use for a merely open episode. This takes precedence over the valuation reason. | `The source record cannot support a money claim for this selection.` |
| `TECHNICAL_CLAIM_LIMITED` (reason) | Confirm for `WITHHELD` when the selected episode cannot prove an eligible exposure lower bound because of a technical claim limit, including contradictory evidence, claim level `POSSIBLE`/`UNKNOWN`, or coverage below 0.8. Use when the source gate passes; an unknown upper bound alone does not trigger it. | `The available temperature evidence cannot establish an exposure amount.` |
| `OPEN_EPISODE_UPPER_UNKNOWN` (limitation) | Confirm whenever `claim.open=true` on a selected eligible excursion. Set consequence `quantity_high=NULL` even if W2-03's observed-segment high is finite. Keep that observed high in the W2-03 technical read. | `The excursion has no recorded end, so its total exposure has no upper bound.` |
| `CONTRADICTORY_EVIDENCE` (limitation) | Confirm only when W2-03 marks the selected claim contradictory. Do not infer failure. | `Accepted records disagree; this conclusion cannot be established.` |
| `CLAIM_LEVEL_POSSIBLE`, `CLAIM_LEVEL_UNKNOWN` (limitations) | Confirm these two fixed expansions of `CLAIM_LEVEL_<level>` when W2-03 selects that level; do not emit `CLAIM_LEVEL_OBSERVED`, `CLAIM_LEVEL_BOUNDED` or a dynamic/unrecognised suffix. `CONTRADICTORY` uses `CONTRADICTORY_EVIDENCE`. | `The evidence suggests an excursion but does not confirm it.` / `The evidence cannot establish whether an excursion occurred.` |
| `COVERAGE_INCOMPLETE` (limitation) | Confirm only when selected qualifying probe coverage is below 0.8; show the actual known/expected points alongside it. A separate >10-minute gap remains a W2-03 gap limit even if coverage is >=0.8. | `Too few qualifying temperature readings were recorded for an exposure conclusion.` |
| `EXPOSURE_LOWER_BOUND_UNKNOWN` (limitation) | Confirm when no defensible `degC_min` lower bound exists; store `quantity_low=NULL`, never substitute zero. | `The recorded readings do not establish a minimum thermal exposure.` |
| `SOURCE_CLASS_MIXED`, `SOURCE_CLASS_UNKNOWN` (limitations) | Confirm these two fixed expansions of `SOURCE_CLASS_<class>` only for those selected aggregate classes. Do not emit an arbitrary class suffix or treat `REGISTERED_SIMULATION`/`AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION` as a technical limitation. | `This selection mixes source types, so its money claim is withheld.` / `The source type is unknown, so its money claim is withheld.` |
| `COLD_RECOMPUTE_FAILED` (worker error) | Confirm as fallback for an unexpected derivation failure after a work item is scoped; retain diagnostic detail in internal logs. Do not mask specific basis/store errors. | If exposed as pending status: `The exposure calculation failed and is waiting to retry.` |
| `STORE_UNAVAILABLE` (worker error) | Confirm for an unavailable consequence/assessment store, matching existing worker vocabulary. A failure to persist a failed attempt cannot be represented as a stored attempt. | If exposed: `The exposure record is temporarily unavailable.` |
| `WORK_SCOPE_MISMATCH` (worker error) | Confirm for a foreign or mismatched work locator. Refuse before derivation and record no attempt against foreign work. | If exposed: `This exposure work item does not belong to the selected site.` |

`COLD_BASIS_MISMATCH` remains the already approved specific worker code. On the six replays, the open excursion retains W2-03's observed rectangle pair in the technical read but produces a consequence with a proved low, null high and `OPEN_EPISODE_UPPER_UNKNOWN`; the normal replay has no exposure row. Assert these separately so a copied finite high cannot pass the consequence test.

## Implementer dry-read: exact backend behavior

| Question a builder would otherwise decide | Binding answer and proof |
| --- | --- |
| Which Finding gets an exposure revision? | Only a selected, warranted `EXCURSION` FindingRevision with an exact `claim.episode_revision_id` and eligible episode. `START_RESPONSE` is chronology/context; do not emit the optional context consequence in W2-04. A successful start, unsupported excursion, or missing-probe episode without a Finding produces no consequence and may close its trigger work with an empty-ID success attempt. This avoids a second counted exposure and an invented zero row. |
| Which assessment is exact? | Join FindingRevision `assessment_revision_id`, `claim.episode_revision_id`, episode revision `assessment_revision_id`, and the selected W2-03 assessment pair by scoped IDs. A mismatch is a typed worker failure `COLD_BASIS_MISMATCH`, never a latest-row fallback. For a source-gate event, re-read the latest settled heads at its transaction snapshot and enqueue only heads whose exact evidence IDs use the linked batch. |
| How is `degC_min` computed? | For a closed eligible episode, copy the selected excursion revision's `degree_minutes_low/high` as decimal strings. For an open eligible episode, copy the proved low and set the consequence high to null regardless of W2-03's finite observed-segment high; include `OPEN_EPISODE_UPPER_UNKNOWN`. Check policy, coverage and evidence eligibility first. Do not integrate an interpolated chart, derive from duration alone or use private trajectory. `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED` has no exposure row; no episode means `UNKNOWN` summary if the selection is otherwise incomplete. |
| How does revision identity work? | Hash the sorted exact accepted IDs with their SUPPORT/CONTEXT/EXCLUDED uses and reasons, Finding/episode/assessment UUIDs and basis hashes, selected window/cutoff, `cold-exposure/1`, quantity bounds, aggregate source class/gate digest, and explicit missing cold assumption markers. Existing identical `(finding_revision_id,kind,basis_sha256)` returns its ID. A changed basis appends `revision_no=max+1` and `prior_revision_id` under the work advisory lock. Never overwrite; reversion to a previously used basis must remain audit-visible, so include the newly selected cutoff/revision ID in the hash. |
| What exactly is pending and when does a work item finish? | Unfinished means no `SUCCEEDED` attempt. Include any relevant PENDING/LEASED/FAILED W2-03 cold assessment job and any cold consequence work with no success; a failed attempt does not clear pending. Relevant means same tenant/Site, overlapping half-open selected window and room. Select work in `(enqueued_at,id)` order, lock its UUID, and use `retry_due_at` from the last attempt; no row status UPDATE. An episode-only no-op success has `[]` IDs. |
| Which source/cutoff gates apply? | Resolve S4-05 aggregate provenance from the exact evidence IDs, not Site mode or a Lab manifest. Recompute after a new publication link. Return historical technical rows at an older explicit `known_at` with the historical label; never call their amount current. Closed disclosure gives 403 before returning restricted accepted IDs; mixed/unknown classification and pending/stale currentness withhold a current money claim. No cold price exists even with coherent simulation. |
| What does `RECORDED_REPAIR` mean here? | It is schema reserved only. Neither a W2-03 start Finding nor mock WR-019 is an accepted, costed work record. No `RECORDED_REPAIR` row, repair amount, currency, incident or maintenance item is synthesized in W2-04. |
| How does the API keep one snapshot? | Authenticate and scope Site first. Open one repeatable-read transaction; capture latest committed S2 disposition watermark once, resolve paired window and optional `known_at`, then call W2-03 read/store, Foundation, source provenance and consequence read methods with the same connection. If W2-03 exposes only HTTP calls, add a caller-connection read method to its existing assessment adapter; never invoke loopback HTTP for composition or import a SQL driver into `cold_site_reads.py`. |
| What is a valid explicit selection/cursor? | `from,to,known_at` are strict UTC instants; both window bounds or neither, `from<to`, at most 31 days, no unknown query keys. `known_at` cannot exceed the captured committed watermark for claims; a future cutoff is 422 `COLD_TIME_INVALID`. Cursor stores exact canonical selection/basis and `(source_time,evidence_id)`; a well-formed cursor for another selection or changed basis is 409. Page after the tuple, never by offset. Empty selected evidence is 200 `UNKNOWN`; unavailable/corrupt storage is 503, not empty. |
| What do `as_of` and contact mean? | For an accepted record, `as_of=received_at` (with separate `source_time`); for assessment/episode/Finding, use immutable revision `created_at`; for consequence use its `created_at`; for a configured policy use effective revision time; for a missing value use selected `known_at` and a limitation. Source contact uses only an identified source-specific diagnostic at/before cutoff; otherwise `UNKNOWN`, even if a gateway is online. Accepted fact age is `to - latest qualifying source_time`, separate from contact. |
| Where do exact links go? | Only emit a clickable target after an integration test proves the target route renders that exact ID with Site, window and cutoff preserved. For an unproved cold record or assessment target, display the exact ID as labelled text plus `Analysis view not available for this cold record`; Finding and Site Evidence links that pass their own route proof remain clickable. Never turn a mock Action, Incident, Maintenance or unbuilt Cooling destination into a link. |

Use `cold_site_reads.py` as a pure composition service, and put SQL in the existing assessment/consequence adapters. `financials/cold_recompute.py` receives store protocols and no SQL driver. The five GETs are composed in `main.py`; do not change the mini-grid routers. Run architecture/dependency, workflow, writer guard and targeted import probes after each owning commit. The review packet states the exact migration SHA, W2-03 predecessor revision, replay manifest IDs, accepted IDs, selected revision IDs and test database name.

# Workflow

AssetOps is built through thin end-to-end slices that produce UI-verifiable
product behavior.

## Lanes

Planned lane:

Architect or Planner -> Implementer -> Reviewer -> User review

Use for new product behavior, domain semantics, user-visible analytics,
meaningful UI/UX, data/API contracts, architecture boundaries, simulator or
ingestion semantics, and external integrations.

Fast lane:

Implementer -> Reviewer

Use only when the work stays inside an existing product and technical contract.

## No Agent Writes An Approval

Recorded 2026-09-26 after an Implementer wrote two approvals that had not
happened into the durable record: a dated owner sign-off whose only basis was
four conversational words, and a review verdict of ACCEPT while a RETURN verdict
sat unread in the same directory. It then set the task complete and moved it to
`tasks/completed/`. Reverted at `166c02b`.

Relying on an agent to be honest about this is what failed, so these are
controls rather than advice.

- **An agent may not write an owner approval anywhere.** Not the task file, not
  a packet, not a commit message. An agent cannot observe the owner's approval;
  only the coordinator, relaying the user, records one. A conversational remark
  is not a checkpoint: the task file defines what the owner must have done, and
  nothing short of that is a sign-off.
- **An agent may not record a review verdict.** The coordinator records it,
  after reading the review file. A subagent's handback is that subagent's
  summary of its own work; the harness says so on every handback, and it was
  said on the one that was believed here.
- **An Implementer may not commission its own review.** A review it
  commissioned is not independent whatever it concludes, and
  `.ai/ROLE_CONFIG.md` requires independence from the author. The coordinator
  dispatches the Reviewer.
- **Before recording any verdict, list the review files.** Two sat in `.agent/`
  that day, twenty-two minutes apart, and the one that agreed was cited. Where
  there is more than one, they are all read and the disagreement is reported,
  not resolved by preference.
- **Do not commit into a tree a Reviewer is measuring.**
  `.ai/PROJECT_RULES.md` "One Working Copy" already says evidence taken while
  another agent holds the tree is not evidence. Four commits landed during that
  review, and it had to qualify its own findings as a result.

A task reaching `tasks/completed/` with a verdict nothing verified is the
failure these prevent. `T011B` and `T011C` reached main at `in_review` for the
mirror-image reason - no check enforces the transition, which is why the rule
has to be read rather than relied upon.

## Vertical Slice Standard

Each planned task should include the minimum required domain semantics, backend
logic, data contract, UI, tests, and demo evidence needed for a reviewable
result. Not every task needs every layer, but each task should either deliver a
UI-verifiable outcome or explicitly unlock a named UI-verifiable slice.

Features and tasks are different planning objects. A feature is a complete
product function that may require several tasks. A task is a reviewable
implementation slice that delivers or materially advances one UI-verifiable part
of a feature.

The current sequence is the Site-first S1â€“S7 queue in `tasks/README.md`, based on the current architecture, technical design and Site-first execution plan. Earlier feature maps and demo roadmaps are historical planning input.

This replaces the old examples that implied a fuel discrepancy was the first
Finding. Within each outcome, create reviewable cuts that let the owner run
and judge progress; do not make each architecture noun a separate task.

Avoid building whole layers before there is useful product behavior.

## Task Spec Size

Task specs are guardrails for fast implementation, not miniature design
documents. For S1-S7, keep durable reasoning in the current Site-first architecture, technical design, execution plan or a new decision; keep task files slice-specific and executable. The old feature map and T0xx queue remain historical.

Size is a ceiling, never a target. These are the upper limits:

- Normal UI/content slice: 120 lines.
- Boundary-changing slice: 160 lines.
- First write path, evidence contract, ingestion, simulator truth boundary, or
  analytics/finding semantics: 220 lines.

**There is no minimum.** A spec that carries everything below in fewer lines is
finished, not thin. Revised 2026-09-24: the earlier 100-180 / 180-260 / 250-400
bands described specs that restated durable mechanics, and their lower bounds
were read as a quota. Specs that cite v4, the roadmap, the feature map,
`.ai/ARCHITECTURE.md` and `.ai/DECISIONS.md` instead of restating them land far
below those numbers and are better for it. Over a ceiling, split the slice or
move the reasoning to the document that owns it.

Line count does not measure whether a spec works. This does: **an implementer
holding the task file, the references it names and the built state can start
without asking a question.** Where they must legitimately stop - a decision only
the user can take, or content that does not exist yet - the file names the stop
rather than leaving the gap silent. A file that passes that test at 70 lines
passes; a file that fails it at 300 fails.

Every task spec should include:

- User-visible outcome.
- Why this slice is next.
- Acceptance criteria tied to behavior or contracts.
- Only the product/domain semantics needed for this slice.
- Protected seams and exact checks.
- Scope limits.
- Whether user review is required.

Leave to implementation:

- Component factoring.
- Helper names.
- Styling mechanics.
- Test structure.
- Local code organization below established boundaries.

## Avoiding Spec Chaff

When writing or revising task specs, prefer behavior and contract guardrails over
defensive repetition.

- Put inherited exclusions in one shared place and reference them from tasks.
- Repeat a prohibition inside a task only when the slice has a special local
  risk or exception.
- Specify the required property and proof, not the exact implementation
  mechanics, unless the mechanism is itself the protected seam.
- Avoid exact wording requirements unless the user has reviewed that copy as
  product language; otherwise require clear, accessible meaning.
- Avoid telling implementers where to put helpers, how to factor components, or
  how to shape tests below established boundaries.
- Treat a high count of `do not`, `must not`, `never`, or `exactly` as a smell
  during planning review. It may be justified, but it should trigger a trim or a
  move into shared guidance.

## Planning Before Tasks

The Architect establishes feature outcomes and causal prerequisites before the
Planner creates tasks. For the superseded T0xx planning pass, the historical instruction was to use simulator v4 and the
corrected mini-grid roadmap for direction, plus canonical built-state records.
This replaces starting the task sequence from the UI mockups. Layout references
remain presentation guidance after a screen's content and claims are real.

The Architect may wear Product, Architecture, UI/UX, and Domain hats as needed
to produce a feature map. That feature map can then be broken into reviewable
tasks by the Planner under architectural guidance.

## Review Packet

Provide concise evidence:

- Task.
- Outcome delivered.
- Files materially changed.
- Acceptance criteria status.
- Checks run.
- Layout evidence, when the slice is layout-sensitive.
- Known deviations.
- Residual risk.
- User-review focus.

The review packet is evidence, not implementation history.

**For the rest of the simulator milestone, a Reviewer raises three things and
records the rest.** Under `D-2026-09-22-milestone-speed-over-purity`: a defect
that would be expensive to reverse later, a claim that would mislead an
Implementer, and a decision only the user can take. Everything else - naming
that is imperfect but honest, a redundant but true restatement, a vocabulary
that would be better shaped and is not wrong - goes to
`.ai/MILESTONE_REVIEW_BACKLOG.md` as a residual-risk entry rather than
blocking the slice. *This would be better named* is a backlog entry; *this
name is false* is still a finding, because a false name misleads. The complete
review, including a possible refactor, follows milestone completion and proper
testing, and the backlog is what it reads first.

A slice is layout-sensitive when it changes shell layout, dense tables,
intrinsic-width drawings, SVG geometry, tab or subtab treatment, or viewport
behaviour. Such a slice runs `tools/layout-evidence.mjs` and records the exact
command, the base URL when non-default, and the final result line. A missing
precondition is not a pass: when Chrome, the backend or the dev server is
unavailable, record `layout evidence: not run` with the blocking precondition,
and the affected layout claim is unverified rather than green. Every new
measured claim about overflow, visibility or rendered content carries at least
one measurement proving its set is non-empty.

## Closeout

Before a slice is merged, its Review Outcome is recorded in the task file and
its status is set to complete, or the task says explicitly what is still open.
Nothing checks this, so it is the Reviewer's and the user's to hold: T011B and
T011C reached `main` at `in_review`, and the user noticed rather than a guard.

### Coordinator transition for the W1â€“W6 delivery loop

The coordinator owns transitions, not implementation or independent review. A candidate may be selected only after the active wave's owner-approved manifest IDs, core-panel keys, 1â€“5 demo values and K are recorded and the registry, ledger, navigation and workflow guards pass on current inputs. Require a reviewed mapped path from the candidate to an approved core claim, closed technical `requires`, no competing active task, and an S/M/L reviewable packet. `sequenced_after` records history; an `enables` path supplies planning benefit only. Stale inputs or checker failure stop selection. `next-work` is advice with evidence, never approval.

For an active task, the coordinator verifies the branch and packet, requests implementation, then dispatches an independent Reviewer after the tree is stable. List and read every review file before recording an outcome; disagreement is reported. On rejection, return the packet/finding to the Implementer for a bounded fix and fresh review. A second rejection of the same task stops the loop for owner direction. Only the coordinator records the review outcome, changes task status or moves a packet to completed, and only from the underlying review evidence. An agent does not write owner approval; the coordinator records owner words against the named checkpoint.

Before merge, verify all required checks and lineage, the independent review result, task closeout/status, and any `USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED` owner checkpoint. A blocked or unrecorded checkpoint stops merge. The coordinator merges the reviewed branch, records commit/evidence and recalculates the registry/report from the merged tree before selecting another task. Do not commit into a Reviewer-measured tree. A real claim without accepted source lineage stops the loop.

At wave start and exit, S2/S4/S6/S7 checkpoints, panel withholding, screen dropping, XL escalation, scoring-parameter change or contested â‰¤5% top-two ranking, stop for the owner with the actual options/evidence. At >5% through 10%, apply the published tie-break, record the two scores and reason in `.agent/coordinator-handoff.md`, and continue; above 10% or with one eligible candidate, continue without ranking notice. Other gates remain binding. A changed input invalidates prior report/selection until guards rerun. The coordinator does not infer an approval from silence or a proposed tier.

## User Review

Use `USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true` when the slice materially affects product
direction, domain semantics, UI/UX, evidence interpretation, user-visible
analytics, information hierarchy, or demo narrative.

After a user-review checkpoint, do not assume the next planned slice is still
correct. The user may approve, revise, reorder, remove, split, expand, or
redirect later work.

Plug assigns the deterministic scoped session ID; authorization lasts one minute; accepted charging emits START. Denial emits no START. Active fault emits FAULT+ABORT; pre-START fault emits FAULT only. Explicit unplug after active charge and without fault emits END. Grid loss pauses power; it never fabricates ABORT. Clear requires forcing. Available shared kW is `max(0,80-base_load)`, allocated in 1 kW round-robin quanta by connector ID with persistent pointer, bounded by request, connector and charger ratings; exact decimal `energy_next=energy+allocated_kw/60`. Once a feed is declared, every declared meter must have exactly that feed as its parent and every declared charger must have exactly one path through a meter from that feed; an unfed meter or charger refuses `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. Any nonempty connector service schedule requires a complete feed→meter→charger→connector path, even when the Site has no feed.

.agent/W2-05a-build-spec.md-36-**Q4, cadence.** `ev-status-cadence` on each connector converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 1`; it is the published state-channel cadence. `ev-meter-cadence` converts min to seconds and must be `0 < value <= 5`; it governs connector energy, connector power, Site import, requested power and allocated limit, all of which publish aligned 300-second *maximum* slots for this profile; the fixture declares exactly 5 min. .agent/W2-05a-build-spec.md:38:**Q5, schedule.** Add `connector_enable_schedules` to `foundation_parsing.py:FOUNDATION_CONTENT_KEYS` and both parser allowlists through that shared constant; it is not `site_controls`. Exactly one schedule entry per declared connector, including a disabled connector (which has an empty `weekly` array); an enabled connector may have an empty weekly array, meaning no service hours. Each entry's zone equals the Site's IANA `timezone`. The fixture zone is `Etc/UTC`, weekly TUESDAY 08:00–16:00 with offset 0 for both c1/c2, so it recurs on Tuesdays during the revision's effective period, not just 6 October. For a bounded effective interval, enumerate each occurrence and validate offset and real, unambiguous local endpoints throughout that interval. For an open-ended interval, permit only zones whose offset is fixed for all future occurrences (`Etc/UTC` qualifies); a DST zone needs a bounded revision and a successor when its offset changes. Reject overlap, invalid endpoint and missing entry as `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID`. At selected `at`, the effective revision supplies its own schedule; never merge schedules across revisions.

.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md-34-
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md-35-## Selection, projection and capability
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md-36-
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md:37:One `ColdSelection` has `tenant_id,site_id,code,from_at,to_at,known_at,watermark,foundation_revision_ids,binding_revision_ids,assessment_revision_id,finding_revision_ids,source_gate`. `from_at` and `to_at` are UTC and half-open; `known_at` defaults to latest committed disposition watermark captured once at request start. Omitted `from,to` selects W2-03's latest settled cold window; exactly one omitted is 422. The effective Foundation/binding is selected at each evidence source time (W2-03), with the selected policy at the requested window; conflicting/absent effective revision is an explicit limitation. 31 days maximum. No newer assessment, Finding or consequence may be silently mixed with an older `known_at`. A late accepted report makes the current projection `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until a new exact assessment and cold consequence both settle. A selected old cutoff is `HISTORICAL`, even when there is no pending work. Determine probe freshness from the latest qualifying accepted probe `source_time` within `[from,to)` compared with `to`: over 10 minutes is `STALE_SOURCE`. Later wall-clock viewing of the 2026-10-06 replay never alone makes it stale or live. Precedence: overlapping queued/failed newer work -> `PENDING_RECOMPUTE`; explicit cutoff older than latest relevant committed disposition -> `HISTORICAL`; no settled selection/probe or future `to` -> `UNKNOWN`; old last probe -> `STALE_SOURCE`; otherwise `CURRENT`. Money can remain `UNPRICED` within a current technical read; pending/old/unresolved money has its own withholding limit.
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md-38-
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md:39:`ColdSiteReadService` joins, by exact UUIDs, W2-03 `ColdReadService` output, W2-01 Foundation, S4-05 source/publication links and the cold consequence head. It does not use a display-name join or the latest Finding irrespective of window. Capability claims are exactly `TEMPERATURE_POINT,EXCURSION_BOUNDS,BACKUP_START_RESULT,TRANSFER_POSITION,COOLING_RESPONSE,PRODUCT_EXPOSURE`. Each has `state:SUPPORTED|LIMITED|UNAVAILABLE|CONTRADICTORY`, `required_roles:string[]`, `missing_roles:string[]`, `coverage:{known_points:int,expected_points:int,ratio:decimal string}` and `as_of,limitation`. `TEMPERATURE_POINT` requires GOOD calibrated room probe and attested clock; `EXCURSION_BOUNDS` adds policy, two qualifying samples and >=0.8 coverage with no >10-minute bridge; `BACKUP_START_RESULT` requires correlated accepted request/result and the W2-03 causal rules; `TRANSFER_POSITION` needs its own bound transfer report; `COOLING_RESPONSE` needs an accepted compressor state and probe series; `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` means bounded thermal exposure only and requires an eligible excursion. Contradictory accepted facts give `CONTRADICTORY`, not healthy/failure. Source `contact` derives from a diagnostic received no later than selected `known_at` only when that diagnostic actually identifies the source; if only gateway-level contact exists, source contact is `UNKNOWN` with a limitation. `fact_age` is a nonnegative integer number of seconds/null between selected `to` and the last accepted source fact in `[from,to)` at the cutoff; it is distinct from contact. No source contact becomes equipment condition. Panel 3's “next evidence” is simply a named missing role/link, never a scored recommendation.
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md-40-
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md-41-Analysis rows are selected accepted public records from the pinned assessment basis and exact links, ordered `(source_time,evidence_id)`, with `evidence_id,kind,source_time,received_at,quality,role,exclusion,source_id,envelope_sha256,assessment_revision_id`; `exclusion` is W2-03's SUPPORT/CONTEXT/EXCLUDED reason or null. Keep a record in the list when excluded, label why, and link to the existing Site Evidence exact record when source gate permits. `claim_rules` lists rule ID `cold-excursion/1` or `cold-start-response/1`, requirement, observed/missing role, coverage and resulting limit, all derived from the selected assessment; no speculative prose rule. Opaque cursor encodes `(source_time,evidence_id)` and selected basis SHA; mismatch returns 409 rather than changing pages.

.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md-43-## Money schema `0023` and rules
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md-44-
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md-45-Create four new tables (do not widen the mini-grid `financial_consequence_revision`, whose CHECK constrains `POTENTIAL_FUEL_COST`, `fuel-cost-1`, L and priced amount). The Site FK uses `(tenant_id,site_id)` -> `site(tenant_id,id)`; references to FindingRevision, cold episode revision, assessment revision and accepted evidence use composite `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` keys; the source link uses `(tenant_id,site_id,batch_id)`. Add scoped unique targets to referenced tables only where `0022` has not already supplied them. All tables have `tenant_id,site_id`; every immutable row has server `created_at`/`finished_at`, with UPDATE/DELETE refusal triggers. Downgrade refuses while any of these rows exist; otherwise drops only W2-04 triggers/tables/indexes, including its added scoped unique target on the older link table.
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md-46-
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md:47:* `cold_consequence_revision`: `id uuid PK`, `finding_revision_id`, `episode_revision_id`, `assessment_revision_id` UUID NOT NULL; `revision_no int >0`, `prior_revision_id uuid NULL`; `kind` CHECK `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE|RECORDED_REPAIR`; `state` CHECK `UNPRICED|WITHHELD`; `reason` nonempty; `window_start,window_end timestamptz` with start<end; `room_asset_id uuid`; `overlap_group text`; `quantity_unit` CHECK `degC_min|none`; `quantity_low,quantity_high numeric NULL` nonnegative/ordered; `amount_low,amount_high numeric NULL`, `currency char(3) NULL`; `finding_basis_sha256,consequence_basis_sha256 char(64)`, `model_id` CHECK `cold-exposure/1|cold-repair/1`; `source_classification`, `source_gate_sha256 char(64)`, `assumption_revision_ids jsonb` array, `limitation text NULL`. Unique `(finding_revision_id,kind,revision_no)`, `(finding_revision_id,kind,consequence_basis_sha256)` and scoped `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` for evidence links. DB CHECK requires `amount_low IS NULL AND amount_high IS NULL AND currency IS NULL` for every row; a later approved migration must widen this. `quantity_unit='none'` requires both quantity bounds null; `degC_min` allows null bounds only when the technical claim is limited/withheld. No `RECORDED_REPAIR` row is created for the six replays because no exact accepted cost/work feed exists. `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` may carry nonnegative bounded `degC_min`; if insufficient evidence both bounds null. No zero amount or stock count stands for unknown.
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md-48-* `cold_consequence_evidence`: `consequence_revision_id,evidence_id,use,reason` with PK `(consequence_revision_id,evidence_id,use)`, `use=SUPPORT|CONTEXT|EXCLUDED` and scoped FKs. Include all exact evidence cited by the pinned Finding/episode basis, no private world keys.
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md-49-* `cold_consequence_work`: `id uuid PK`, `cause` CHECK `FINDING_REVISION_APPENDED|EPISODE_REVISION_APPENDED|SOURCE_GATE_CHANGED`; nullable `finding_revision_id`, `episode_revision_id`, `publication_batch_id`, with a CHECK requiring exactly the locator for its cause, and scoped FKs; `window_start,window_end` (nonempty, half-open), `room_asset_id`, `dirty_key_sha256 char(64)`, `enqueued_at`; unique `(tenant_id,site_id,dirty_key_sha256)` and `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` for attempts. Add `(tenant_id,site_id,batch_id)` uniqueness to `batch_publication_link` in 0023 for the scoped `publication_batch_id` FK; `batch_id` is that table's actual PK, there is no link `id`. Trigger-only INSERT with `ON CONFLICT (tenant_id,site_id,dirty_key_sha256) DO NOTHING`. A cold FindingRevision or episode revision enqueues its selected Site/window/room; the source-gate trigger enqueues only overlapping heads. No runtime work insert/status update. Backfill existing W2-03 cold heads once during upgrade, idempotently. `0010_financial_assumptions` has a diesel-only DB CHECK as well as a diesel-only publisher: **no `ASSUMPTION_PUBLISHED` cause or trigger in 0023**. A future cold assumption contract must migrate the DB, publisher and dirty-work cause together.
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md-50-* `cold_consequence_work_attempt`: `id uuid PK`, `work_id` scoped FK, `attempt_no int >0`, `outcome` CHECK `SUCCEEDED|FAILED`, `error_code text NULL`, `consequence_revision_ids jsonb` array, `finished_at`; unique `(work_id,attempt_no)`. Success has no error and exact returned IDs; failure has error and empty IDs. A failed attempt leaves work pending; pending means no successful attempt. Index `(site_id,enqueued_at)` and `(work_id,attempt_no DESC)`.
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md-51-
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md:52:`overlap_group = 'cold:' + sha256(canonical JSON array [tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,room_asset_id])`: literal lowercase `cold:` prefix, then 64 lowercase hex characters; it is a grouping label, not a V4 event/correlation ID (whose grammar excludes `:`). An excursion and backup Finding of the same selected room/window share it; the financial read groups `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` by this key and takes the single selected excursion exposure basis, never sums both. The start-response Finding may carry a `WITHHELD` context consequence with the same key but zero *counted* exposure entries, not a numeric zero. No cross-window summing without an explicit disjoint-window contract.
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md-53-
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md:54:The source-gate event is an appended `batch_publication_link` to a previously accepted cold batch, if one exists; its trigger selects only cold heads whose linked accepted IDs use that batch and enqueues `SOURCE_GATE_CHANGED` inside the link transaction. An initial link before acceptance has no heads and creates no money work; later W2-03 Finding/episode append enqueues it. `evidence_publication` is immutable and a new unrelated publication never dirties an existing cold basis. Each trigger derives the same canonical dirty key from `(cause,locator,tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,room_asset_id)` and takes a Site advisory transaction lock before selecting heads, following `0012_evidence_consequence_work.py`'s enqueue lock pattern. The FindingRevision trigger is restricted to `NEW.domain='cold_chain'`. An episode with no warranted Finding still enqueues; the worker records a successful no-op attempt with an empty consequence ID array. No trigger on an accepted disposition bypasses W2-03 assessment: it waits for the new exact cold Finding/episode revision.
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md-55-
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md:56:`cold-exposure/1` derives observed rectangle degree-minute bounds only from W2-03's persisted episode bounds/accepted evidence, not a continuous curve. A bounded positive or zero technical quantity is allowed only when W2-03 actually proved it; missing-probe/contradictory has null quantity. `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` is unpriced for sound technical bounds because no `cold_stock_quantity`, `cold_stock_unit_value`, reviewed `cold_exposure_rule`, product/currency binding or source-gated valuation exists. These are **reserved future scenario keys**, displayed as missing, not publishable via the diesel-price publisher. Product value/currency and EV tariff would be explicitly authored scenario assumptions and still would not prove sales, collections, spoilage or lost revenue. Recorded repair needs an accepted exact cost/currency/work record; none of the six replay cases has one. Mixed/unknown source, closed S4-05 gate, stale/pending assessment, incomplete period or contradictory evidence yields `WITHHELD` and null amount. LR current-money gate also refuses a current money claim from historical/pending basis. `exact_basis` returns selected Finding/episode/assessment UUIDs, accepted evidence UUIDs, source gate digest, assumption revision UUIDs (empty in W2) and consequence basis SHA. Basis SHA hashes canonical sorted IDs, window/cutoff, model/formula version, quantity bounds, source gate and missing assumption markers; an identical basis returns the existing ID and a changed basis appends a new revision.
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md-57-
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md-58-`PostgresConsequenceStore` alone inserts `cold_consequence_revision`, `cold_consequence_evidence` and `cold_consequence_work_attempt`; add separate cold methods without changing the existing mini-grid methods. `cold_recompute.process_one` selects unfinished work under `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('cold_consequence:' || work_id,0))`, takes one repeatable-read snapshot, refuses a foreign/missing exact basis, writes consequence/links and `SUCCEEDED` attempt in one transaction. On derivation failure it rolls back that transaction and appends a typed `FAILED` attempt in a fresh transaction. A crash before success commits leaves no partial revision; retries are idempotent. `run` uses the existing `financials/recompute.py:retry_due_at` 1,2,4,8,16-minute schedule and bounded batch; one bad work item cannot starve others. Trigger-created work does not depend on a successful worker.

.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md-63-
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md:64:Refusal code contract (same scope and parse semantics as `cold_assessment_api.py:build_cold_assessment_router`): 401 `UNAUTHENTICATED`; 403 `SITE_SCOPE_FORBIDDEN` or `COLD_SOURCE_DISCLOSURE_FORBIDDEN`; 404 `SITE_NOT_FOUND`; 409 `COLD_SELECTION_UNSETTLED` for an explicitly requested revision that has no settled exact basis or `COLD_CURSOR_BASIS_CHANGED` for a changed Analysis basis; 422 `COLD_TIME_INVALID`, `COLD_WINDOW_INVALID`, `COLD_WINDOW_TOO_LONG`, `COLD_PARAMETER_UNKNOWN`, `COLD_LIMIT_INVALID`, or `COLD_CURSOR_INVALID`; 503 `COLD_READ_UNAVAILABLE`. A wrong-type/foreign Site is 404 after access is checked. An absent optional current basis is 200 `UNKNOWN`, not a 409. Analysis cursor is unpadded base64url of sorted, compact UTF-8 JSON `{basis:<64 lowercase hex>,id:<UUID string>,t:<UTC instant>}`; malformed is 422 and an exact basis mismatch is 409. No arbitrary `after` offset or page across a changed basis.

For an `EXCURSION`, `cold_episode.asset_id` is the room. Use UTC `Z` timestamps with six fractional digits **when microseconds are nonzero**, and whole seconds otherwise. This selected-window key is distinct from `source_envelope.instant_text`, which emits whole seconds for envelope publication; W2-03's `_instant` accepts fractional selected bounds. Reject precision beyond microseconds at the API boundary; never truncate a selected window to seconds when hashing. The six replay windows happen to have whole seconds. SQL and Python golden vectors must match for whole-second and fractional inputs.

W2-03 can persist both observed rectangle degree-minute bounds while `claim.open=true`, `duration_high=NULL` and both end bounds are null. For every selected open excursion with a proved lower bound, store `quantity_low=episode.degree_minutes_low`, `quantity_high=NULL`, `state=UNPRICED` when no other withholding gate applies, and include `OPEN_EPISODE_UPPER_UNKNOWN`. The W2-03 finite observed-segment high must not become a high for total episode exposure. Apply this based on open/end state, not `episode.degree_minutes_high IS NULL`; the canonical excursion replay must carry the limit. For a closed, eligible excursion copy both proved bounds. `WITHHELD` may have either/both null bounds. Null amount/currency remains mandatory in every case.

.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md:116:7. **Q7 CHANGE — constrain the aggregate.** `source_classification` is exactly `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION|REGISTERED_SIMULATION|MIXED|UNKNOWN`, the values returned by `evidence/source_provenance.py`; add a SQL CHECK. `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` is not a current class. .agent/W2-04-build-spec.md-117-8. **Q8 ACCEPT.** Missing room or invalid assessment window is an invariant violation: raise and roll back the W2-03 assessment/Finding transaction, following `0012` rather than silently dropping dirty work. The worker reports `FAILED` for later derivation faults; it cannot repair a missing enqueue.

Include any relevant PENDING/LEASED/FAILED W2-03 cold assessment job and any cold consequence work with no success; a failed attempt does not clear pending. Relevant means same tenant/Site, overlapping half-open selected window and room. Select work in `(enqueued_at,id)` order, lock its UUID, and use `retry_due_at` from the last attempt; no row status UPDATE. An episode-only no-op success has `[]` IDs. |

.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md:154:| What is a valid explicit selection/cursor? | `from,to,known_at` are strict UTC instants; both window bounds or neither, `from<to`, at most 31 days, no unknown query keys. `known_at` cannot exceed the captured committed watermark for claims; a future cutoff is 422 `COLD_TIME_INVALID`. Cursor stores exact canonical selection/basis and `(source_time,evidence_id)`; a well-formed cursor for another selection or changed basis is 409. Page after the tuple, never by offset. Empty selected evidence is 200 `UNKNOWN`; unavailable/corrupt storage is 503, not empty. |
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md:155:| What do `as_of` and contact mean? | For an accepted record, `as_of=received_at` (with separate `source_time`); for assessment/episode/Finding, use immutable revision `created_at`; for consequence use its `created_at`; for a configured policy use effective revision time; for a missing value use selected `known_at` and a limitation. Source contact uses only an identified source-specific diagnostic at/before cutoff; otherwise `UNKNOWN`, even if a gateway is online. Accepted fact age is `to - latest qualifying source_time`, separate from contact. |
.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md:156:| Where do exact links go? | Only emit a clickable target after an integration test proves the target route renders that exact ID with Site, window and cutoff preserved. --
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3031-- **Independent version.** `SOURCE_ENVELOPE_SCHEMA_VERSION` (1) does not move
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3032-  with `EXECUTION_CONTRACT_VERSION` (8) and vice versa. The execution contract
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3033-  says what a frozen Draft means when it runs; the envelope schema says what a
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3034-  published message looks like. The persisted stage document has its own
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3035-  `stage_schema_version` (2 since the T023 fix round added the publication
.ai/DECISIONS.md:3036:  watermark; 1 never left the branch) for the same reason.
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3037-- **Compatibility is equality.** A parser of version 1 refuses any other
.ai/DECISIONS.md:3038:  version as `SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED` and never reads it "as close enough". A later
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3039-  version that must accept version-1 envelopes does so by an explicit reader of
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3040-  version 1, not by loosening the check.
.ai/DECISIONS.md:3041:- **Exact key sets at every level and no free-text field.** An unknown field is
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3042-  refused wherever it appears. Identities are validated identifiers, categories
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3043-  are closed vocabularies (`STATE_AT_INSTANT`/`INTERVAL_MEAN`,
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3044-  `GOOD`/`UNAVAILABLE`, `NO_COMPLETED_INTERVAL`/`NO_MEASUREMENT`), values are
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3045-  canonical exact decimal text, times are UTC instants. This is what makes the
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3046-  truth barrier structural at the boundary: a private field has nowhere to go.
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3047-- **The message id is derived, and content is not in it.** BLAKE2b-256 over
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3048-  Site, gateway, source, device, signal and source time, recomputed by the
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3049-  parser. A retry carries the same id by construction; same id with different
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3050-  content is a conflict the stage refuses rather than an overwrite.
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3051-- **`received_at` is not a field.** It is ingestion's (Evidence Loop
.ai/DECISIONS.md:3052:  Boundaries), and a document carrying one is refused as an unknown field.
.ai/DECISIONS.md:3053:- **A sample that was never published has no envelope.** A reporting gap or a
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3054-  dropped publication produces nothing; only a sample that was taken and had no
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3055-  value may produce a typed `UNAVAILABLE` envelope, and only where the mapping
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3056-  says the signal publishes unavailability. `STALE` is a consumer's conclusion
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3057-  and is not an envelope quality.
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3058-- **Mappings are versioned and frozen into the stage.** The stage persists the
.ai/DECISIONS.md:3059:  whole mapping it was staged under and is read under that mapping, never the
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3060-  current one. The shipped allowlist is the fuel level sensor alone
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3061-  (`simulator-lab-gateway-fuel` v1); T026 extends it.

.ai/DECISIONS.md-3365-
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3366-**The rule.** A run binds a scenario version to a site version. A scenario
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3367-version declares the contract a site must honour - the addresses and sources it
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3368-acts on and reads, the site controls it relies on, and (later) checked purpose
.ai/DECISIONS.md:3369:assumptions - and never names a site, not even as a default. A site never names
.ai/DECISIONS.md:3370:a scenario. The user chooses the site at run setup. Run setup never infers,
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3371-substitutes or defaults either side; it evaluates the contract (document, the
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3372-chosen model profile's bindings, assumptions) against the chosen site, freezes
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3373-what it found, and rewrites neither document. Whether a site can honour a
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3374-scenario is asked of a (scenario version, profile version) pair.

.ai/DECISIONS.md-3882-
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3883-Resolve `A-edge-batch-signature` by **deferring per-batch cryptographic signatures and key rotation for this pilot**. Batch V2 keeps registered TLS gateway credentials, canonical SHA-256 payload digest and exact-byte retry. A future security requirement may activate signing only after amending the technical design, Batch V2 wire contract and test vectors. Signing implementation nodes remain open outside the S1–S7 queue; this decision does not claim they are built.
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3884-
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3885-The S1-02 gateway configuration snapshot is versioned, content-hashed and delivered over an authenticated channel in this queue. Earlier "signed snapshot" wording does not require detached cryptographic signing or key rotation. A later decision to require that must define keys, verification and rotation explicitly.

.ai/DECISIONS.md-3960-
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3961-Decision: `D-2026-10-09-s2-simulated-publication-clock`. Codex Architect, resolving S2-01c Planner Q1 in `.agent/architect-S2-01c-answers.md`. Select an explicitly advanced, persisted virtual publication clock for each registered Lab run. It is a simulated-device publication timeline, distinct from both the run's world-execution progress and the real UTC clock used for gateway diagnostics/server receipt. A world step may advance the publication clock through its reached simulation instant; after world execution completes, the operator/test harness may advance publication time further without rerunning the kernel. Pause and restart preserve the last advanced instant; neither wall-clock passage nor process restart releases a pending report. Advancing to an earlier instant refuses; repeated advancement to the same instant is idempotent.
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3962-
.ai/DECISIONS.md:3963:The Lab-private scheduler persists each delayed public reading, its original source instant, scheduled release instant, stable admission key and pending/released state before gateway submission. The scheduled instant is derived from the frozen delay window and remains private Lab scheduling provenance, not V3/Batch V2/GatewayDiagnostics data. At or after the due instant, the authenticated host advances the gateway's scoped per-run virtual publication clock and submits the unchanged public reading. The gateway owns and persists that clock and stamps `published_at` with its current virtual instant at first admission/sealing; if advancement skips past the scheduled instant, the actual virtual admission instant is used rather than backdating to the schedule. Require `published_at >= source_time`. Retry/restart reuse the same report key and stored envelope/batch bytes, never re-stamp or re-sequence. A pending report has no V3 envelope or sequence; it is visible in Lab as pending with source and scheduled instants. The gateway receives no private delay cause or oracle.
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3964-

For SIMULATED evidence, `published_at - source_time` is a simulated reporting delay; `received_at - published_at` is not a physical transfer-lag measure when the clocks use different timelines. S2-02 must label these domains, display source/published/received instants with provenance and derive central diagnostic freshness from server receive time, not virtual Lab time. A future LIVE gateway uses its own real clock and does not inherit this Lab control.

.ai/DECISIONS.md-3967-Decision: `D-2026-10-09-s2-gateway-diagnostic-semantics`. Codex Architect, resolving S2-01c phase-1 Q1-Q5 in `.agent/architect-S2-01c-answers-2.md`; this is a contract clarification, not an implementation review. Keep `GatewayDiagnosticsV1` pressure provenance off the V1 wire: `disk_pressure` is the gateway's operative `OK|WARN|STOP` level, while the local read identifies `MEASURED|INJECTED_FAULT_FIXTURE|UNMEASURABLE`. A simulated gateway's central STOP is not measured hardware pressure. At STOP, all new acquisition calls, including replays, refuse before writes or sequence allocation; sealing existing admitted envelopes and emitting diagnostics may continue. A failed free-space probe is locally `UNMEASURABLE` and operatively STOP. The default 60-second cadence and 1 GiB/256 MiB free-space WARN/STOP thresholds are frozen, provisioned demo values, not universal physical safety limits.
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3968-
.ai/DECISIONS.md:3969:Persist at most one immutable diagnostic per declared real-UTC cadence slot; a repeat returns its exact stored identity and bytes, and a clock earlier than the latest slot refuses. State changes within a slot are visible in the local read immediately but reach the wire in the next slot; the server uses receive time and cadence to decide central freshness. `queue_count` is the number of `STAGED|READY|IN_FLIGHT` envelopes, sealed or unbatched, excluding holds and dead letters; queue age uses their real-UTC outbox creation time and must remain real UTC when Lab V3 `published_at` becomes virtual. The local read retains sealed/unbatched/held/refused/dead-letter breakdown. `mapping_error_count` is the number of distinct `mapping_hold` rows with kind in `{NO_ACTIVE_MAPPING, UNKNOWN_CHANNEL, SOURCE_NOT_PERMITTED, OUTSIDE_VALIDITY, UNIT_INCOMPATIBLE, INVALID_ENUM}`; it is not the total held-report count. Contact is gateway real-UTC observation of a public report that resolves to a permitted source, even on replay or later hold; never-heard remains null. For a SIMULATED gateway its provenance is `OBSERVED_SIMULATED_REPORT`, never proof of physical controller contact or source health. S2-02 carries these meanings into authenticated receipt and central presentation without adding cause or private Lab fields to V1.

.ai/DECISIONS.md-3978-
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3979-For a `RUN_VIRTUAL` profile, ordinary undelayed readings publish in source-boundary order at their own reached virtual instant, with `published_at == source_time` for the current cadence-aligned profile. A world step that spans many boundaries, including `run_to_end`, must process the same ordered boundary sequence as single-boundary steps: advance the gateway clock, admit that boundary's undelayed reports, and release delayed reports at the first reached boundary at or after due time. Retries return prior bytes; no unadmitted earlier ordinary report may be silently stamped with a later clock merely because batching, restart or a fault skipped its boundary. .ai/DECISIONS.md-3983-Decision: `D-2026-10-09-s2-publication-gap-and-batch-boundary`. Codex Architect, resolving S2-01c fix re-review R1 and questions-5 in `.agent/architect-S2-batch-answers-2026-10-09.md`. A completed `RUN_VIRTUAL` schedule's `admitted_through` is the last contiguous ordinary-publication boundary. If it is absent or earlier than `world_reached_instant` after a failed pass, a **stored-schedule-only** advance must refuse `PUBLICATION_GAP` before changing either Lab or gateway clock or submitting a due delayed report. A live session may re-drive the missing ordinary boundary while its gateway clock still permits its original instant. If that instant has passed, keep `PUBLICATION_INSTANT_PASSED`, stop later ordinary and delayed admissions for that run, and show the terminal gap; recovery is a new run, not late stamping or a hidden sequence jump. This strengthens, rather than replaces, the earlier rule permitting a delayed report to publish at the next actually admitted boundary or explicit post-run instant when no ordinary gap blocks it.

.ai/DECISIONS.md-3991-Decision: `D-2026-10-09-s2-terminal-publication-gap-closeout`. Codex Architect, resolving the merged S2-01c questions-6 in `.agent/architect-S2-batch-answers-2.md`. .ai/DECISIONS.md:3993:Decision: `D-2026-10-09-s2-ingestion-operational-semantics`. Codex Architect, resolving S2-02 phase-1 Q1-Q7 and phase-2 Q8-Q12 in `.agent/architect-S2-batch-answers-2.md`. Gateway/source external IDs remain mutable present-day names, while immutable registered UUIDs and the pinned published snapshot spelling determine accepted-as identity; asset code and tenant/Site/gateway/source affiliation remain fixed as implemented. An operator provisioning command may enable an imported gateway and only explicitly named registered sources while issuing its credential; an upload never enables either. Site/gateway/mode/disabled/source-scope mismatches are 403 without a Site-visible ledger row; wrong authenticated configuration references are whole-batch 422 refusals with an audit row, while individual source-time, binding, unit, enum, conversion and identity decisions remain per ordinal.
