Each accepted evidence row and Receipt V2 uses the batch's first server `received_at`; each ordinal's decision time remains separately recorded. New acceptances enqueue/coalesce `dispatch` recompute jobs over their source/interval window; duplicates and rejections enqueue none, and S3 expands neighboring analytic windows as needed.
.ai/DECISIONS.md-3994-
.ai/DECISIONS.md:3995:Pilot bearer rotation revokes prior credential verifiers atomically when the new verifier is committed, with no overlap. The new secret must first be durably placed in a verified protected, versioned gateway file; a file/permission failure must leave the old credential active and the gateway/sources no more enabled than before. Do not overwrite the only usable old secret before successful issue; on Windows, verify an ACL restricting read to the operator and gateway process identity or refuse provisioning rather than claiming POSIX-only mode bits protected it. The central diagnostic is `CURRENT` through two declared-cadence periods from server receipt, then `STALE`, and `UNKNOWN` until a first receipt; this bounds slot-edge jitter without asserting device health. Pilot gateway upload uses direct TLS with the ASGI HTTPS scheme; forwarded scheme headers are not upload authority. A gateway diagnostic with malformed bytes or identity conflict receives an audited 422/409, while wrong path/claimed scope or foreign source receives 403 without a Site ledger row. The provisioning CLI remains development-tenant-only; its OS-user actor is labelled local operator context, not a verified tenant-user identity.

.ai/DECISIONS.md-4029-
.ai/DECISIONS.md:4030:For each candidate `c`, let `D(c)` be the highest owner-set 1–5 demo value among its approved downstream screens (a direct screen task uses that screen's value). Let `d(c,p)` be the fewest **remaining closeouts after c** on a reviewed path from c to an approved core panel `p`, counting distinct architecture, data, read and UI gates needed for that panel; `d=0` means closing c makes that panel ready. Use **every distinct reachable approved core panel once**; there is no five-panel cap. Define discounted breadth `B(c)=Σ_p 1/(1+d(c,p))` and bounded panel leverage **`P(c)=5×(1−exp(−B(c)/K))`**, with **K=10 for W1**. This preserves each additional panel's contribution while keeping `0≤P<5` across waves of different size. At each later wave start, the Architect reviews the approved core-panel count and resulting score spread, proposes K (including 10 if still suitable), and the owner confirms it with that wave's IDs, core panels and demo values; a K change never occurs mid-wave without the owner's scoring-change gate. No reachable approved panel means `P=0` and no inherited demo value, so the candidate needs a documented connection before autonomous selection. Let `R(c)=min(5, distinct downstream subject areas/domains with identified approved consumers)` and `E(c)=1/3/5` for S/M/L. Then **`score(c)=0.4 D(c)+0.3 P(c)+0.2 R(c)−0.1 E(c)`** (bounded from −0.1 to below 4.4 for S/M/L). Publish uncapped reach/panel counts and the path/distance proof; a path cannot skip unresolved AND branches. `ready-now` counts `d=0` panels; `closure-unlock` counts panels that would become ready after c plus already named open upstream gates, reported separately and not added twice. Recompute after each recorded decision, accepted task or closed fixture. Exact score ties use fewer unresolved technical prerequisite gates, then lower effort, then stable node ID. Product-node `priority` is the wave ID, not the score.

.ai/DECISIONS.md-4077-
.ai/DECISIONS.md:4078:**Input and cohort.** `EvidenceQuery` is a tenant-authorized, Site/domain-scoped query for a UTC half-open window at `knowledge_at`; it returns typed normalized accepted readings, original interval/source times, quality, canonical role/asset/unit, exact accepted evidence IDs and content digests, as-accepted Foundation/binding revisions and source/sequence-epoch provenance. It selects by source-time or reported-interval overlap, including bracketing evidence needed to bound continuity, with the chosen knowledge cutoff applied to server acceptance/recording. `AnalyticInputSet` freezes the selected public evidence cohort, role coverage/missingness, effective public configuration, analytic/rule version, window and cutoff; its canonical SHA-256 covers sorted evidence IDs **and digests**, cohort identity, relevant Foundation/binding revision IDs and values, role coverage, window and model version. SQL rows, raw envelope JSON, gateway diagnostics, Lab world/truth, private run IDs and file-backed assessment objects stop before this boundary. `RUN_VIRTUAL` source time is a simulation clock, not real-world contact freshness.
.ai/DECISIONS.md-4079-
.ai/DECISIONS.md:4080:Overlapping accepted simulated reports are separate evidence, not one physical series. The selected basis is an explicit, persisted **public report cohort**: for each required source, registered source UUID and sequence epoch, with the included batch/evidence IDs and common Site/configuration/window basis; the request/read returns that selection. A multi-source cohort must be proven coherent by its public gateway/source-epoch and sealed batch provenance. The private Lab run manifest may guide a demo operator to those public IDs but is not an analytic input or authority. S2's two overlapping runs and labelled `fault-fixture-epoch` must remain separate choices. If there is no unique/provably coherent cohort, return a limited/Indeterminate result naming the ambiguity; never prefer the latest receipt, sum epochs, use the fault fixture as ordinary output or deduplicate distinct evidence by timestamp. Within a selected cohort, same-role/time disagreements remain conflicts rather than arbitrarily choosing a value.
.ai/DECISIONS.md-4081-
.ai/DECISIONS.md-4082-**Dispatch rule.** The first Postgres model is a **new version** of the mini-grid dispatch rule; it may reuse the published `dispatch-2` thresholds/decision table but must name every carried or changed rule in a versioned table and compare its results. Reconstruct generator ON/OFF intervals with observed bounds/gaps. For each segment, apply evidenced constraints in order: an authorized, supported override or effective minimum runtime; battery reserve/near-reserve; PV-plus-battery capability shortfall; then candidate headroom. A whole interval is Necessary only if every assessed segment is Necessary; any supported Candidate segment gives `CANDIDATE_AVOIDABLE` only for those bounded segments; otherwise it is Indeterminate. Missing role, poor quality, contradiction, open bound or ambiguity names the affected segment/limit. Candidate requires accepted **available PV power** and PCS **discharge capability** (the published `dispatch-2` PV-alone branch also requires evidenced PCS availability), plus the load and, for battery-supported headroom, SOC and selected Foundation reserve basis; observed `pv_power` and rated/actual `battery_power` are not substitutes. The optional available-power and discharge-capability roles require a separately published, versioned binding vocabulary and actual registered readings before S3 can make a candidate claim; `minigrid-roles-1` remains historically interpretable. The S1 Foundation's time-valid `battery_reserve` control is authoritative; S2's measurement-only V3 path supplies no accepted POLICY/OPERATIONAL_RECORD override. Do not fabricate one from a Foundation change or Lab event. Missing optional override records do not by themselves block a Candidate, but the result must say no command record was observed, not that no command occurred; any conflicting supported constraint makes the affected portion Indeterminate under the versioned rule. A supported policy/command record later needs its own versioned provenance and conflict contract before it can change this rule. Necessary policy-only claims require that supported record; otherwise classify from evidenced physical constraints or Indeterminate. Dispatch states a technical candidate, not recovered fuel/cost, physical equipment condition, causal intent or a Finding; S4 owns Finding identity and money.
.ai/DECISIONS.md-4083-
.ai/DECISIONS.md:4084:**Revisions and jobs.** S3 consumes the S2 `dispatch` recompute queue transactionally with a lease/retry, expands an accepted record's affected source-time interval to continuity neighbors, and reruns at a recorded cutoff. Backdated Foundation/binding publication and analytic-model changes enqueue affected windows too. The worker persists an idempotent `AnalyticRun` keyed by Site, analytic/model version, window and input hash, its exact evidence-ID set, and append-only `AssessmentRevision` rows under a stable interval/asset assessment key. Identical input and output produce no new revision; a changed evidence/configuration/model basis appends a new revision while preserving the earlier one and its selected read URL. Projection replacement and job completion occur atomically only for the still-current input/watermark; a newly dirtied overlapping window must remain dirty for another pass, never be lost by a leased worker. Late accepted evidence may change Necessary/Candidate/Indeterminate but never rewrites an earlier revision. The Performance list/detail returns selected revision, rule version, input hash, coverage, evidence IDs, source and analytic watermarks, built time and explicit limitations.

Once S3 owns Performance, use `UNASSESSED` when no completed assessment exists, `PENDING` for a first run in progress, `CURRENT` only when a completed projection's input/source watermark matches accepted evidence and no overlapping dirty/leased/failed job remains, `STALE_PROJECTION` when a previous result exists but any such job or watermark mismatch remains, and `FAILED` when computation failed without a usable current result. .ai/DECISIONS.md-4129-
.ai/DECISIONS.md:4130:**Identity and continuity.** Persist one tenant/Site/domain/asset/mechanism-scoped Finding UUID and immutable `finding_key` when the first supported candidate span is recorded. The key is allocated from the persisted episode identity/anchor, not recomputed from a mutable first-ON timestamp, assessment key, model output hash, UTC day or private Lab run. Match later selected candidate spans to that episode across day boundaries when they overlap or are separated by at most the declared 20-minute dispatch continuity tolerance and there is no contradictory/ambiguous basis across the gap. An evidenced separation greater than 20 minutes creates a new Finding linked by `recurs_after_finding_id`; it is not revision 2 of the first. Persist the match rule version, observed bounds, anchor, cohort and selected window(s). A late earlier report can extend the episode without renaming it. If new evidence splits a previously single episode, retain the original ID for the portion containing its immutable anchor, create new linked Finding(s) for other supported portions, and append a revision explaining the changed coverage. If a later bridge joins two already persisted episodes, keep both old URLs/history, choose the earlier stable anchor (then UUID) as canonical, and record an explicit superseded/merged link on the other; never silently collapse or reuse IDs.
.ai/DECISIONS.md-4131-
.ai/DECISIONS.md:4132:**Revisions and lifecycle.** Each changed selected assessment basis, supported candidate span, limitation or rule version appends an immutable FindingRevision under the same episode only when its canonical Finding basis changes; retry of the same basis is idempotent. A revision cites the exact selected interval AssessmentRevision and its AnalyticRun/model version, plus the selected window AssessmentRevision and every additional interval revision used for a cross-window episode, with exact accepted evidence IDs/digests and time-valid Foundation/binding provenance. Older `dispatch-3` results may be pinned as history but cannot create or refresh a CURRENT Finding under `dispatch-3.1`; later rules create new FindingRevisions, never relabel old ones. A CURRENT complete assessment proving the candidate span no longer supported appends a `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` revision and closes the active candidate claim, without asserting operational repair or savings. A stale, failed, ambiguous or Indeterminate assessment instead leaves the prior revision inspectable but marks current support unconfirmed; it does not auto-close. Reappearance after a proven separated interval creates a recurrence Finding and link. Model the close/reopen/split/merge transition as durable recorded history, not an overwritten claim.

.ai/DECISIONS.md-4199-
.ai/DECISIONS.md:4200:Action state is derived from an append-only, server-ordered `ActionEvent` ledger. Record stable event ID, Action and Site, event type, `occurred_at`, server `recorded_at`, idempotency key, actor kind/ID, source channel, authorization basis and triggering command/job or content revision. Idempotent identical retries return the event; conflicting reuse and cross-Site references fail. Historical reads select a recorded cutoff and then `(recorded_at,id)` order; a backdated `occurred_at` is displayed as such but never rewrites the audit order. `PROPOSED`/`ACCEPTED`, application `PENDING`/`APPLIED`/`FAILED`, and later verification status are separate dimensions. Acceptance does not assert application; an applied change or completed work does not assert benefit. A failed application preserves its attempt, error and retry relation without an effective-policy claim. No human author, approval or attestation is inferred from a gateway credential, client field, local OS user or SYSTEM fixture. Under the existing `A-actor-identity` rule, human writes need a verified operator principal; otherwise only a clearly labelled, server-authorized SYSTEM demonstration command with stable service/job provenance is allowed, or the write stays unavailable.

.agent/W2-03-build-spec.md:54:4. **Q4, duration:** for an open episode, `duration_low=max(0,last continuously observed out-of-band source_time - latest_start)`. A >10-minute gap stops that continuous run. The half-open window end is a selection boundary, not an observed reading. For the 19:15 to 20:00 canonical run the lower bound is **45 min**, not 50. `duration_high` and both end bounds remain null until a supported return. For a closed episode, lower duration uses the last continuously observed out-of-band point, and upper duration needs both known start and return bounds.
.agent/W2-03-build-spec.md-55-5. **Q5, earliest start:** null unless the preceding qualifying in-band point is no more than 10 minutes before the first out-of-band point. A preceding out-of-band or excluded point cannot supply it.
.agent/W2-03-build-spec.md-56-6. **Q6, levels:** coverage below `0.8` gives assessment/episode `UNKNOWN` and no exposure Finding; conflicting GOOD same-instant probe values give `CONTRADICTORY`. With adequate coverage, a confirmed excursion with lower duration at least 15 min and a permitting gate is `BOUNDED` with an exposure Finding; a shorter confirmed excursion or a closed gate is `POSSIBLE` without one. A lone out-of-band point is assessment state `UNKNOWN`, level `POSSIBLE`. Fully in-band is `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`/`BOUNDED` with no excursion episode or Finding. Do not turn a closed gate into a positive Finding.
.agent/W2-03-build-spec.md-57-7. **Q7, start Finding:** only `OBSERVED_FAILURE` plus permitting gate opens a start-response Finding. An explicit GOOD failure without conflicting GOOD success qualifies; a timeout qualifies only with complete generator-state coverage through its effective deadline. `OBSERVED_SUCCESS`, `UNKNOWN` and `CONTRADICTORY` do not open one.
.agent/W2-03-build-spec.md-58-8. **Q8, transfer:** only a qualifying `TRANSFER_RESULT` before the first `POWER_RESTORED` in the same correlation counts as backup transfer. Bus state can be observed while `TRANSFER` remains `MISSING`; a post-restoration grid transfer cannot close the backup step.
.agent/W2-03-build-spec.md-59-9. **Q9, peak:** for HIGH, `peak_observed=max(qualifying observed temperatures)`; for LOW, `min(...)`. Preserve the direction with the episode result/read. No interpolation or mock value supplies the peak.
.agent/W2-03-build-spec.md-60-10. **Q10, calibration:** resolve calibration while selecting the effective Foundation/`cold-roles-1` binding and primary room probe via `probe_asset_id`; pass only that eligible probe to the pure `ColdEvidence` rule. The pure record has no calibration field. Missing, duplicate or overlapping effective calibration/role periods fail closed.
.agent/W2-03-build-spec.md-61-11. **Q11, contradiction:** link each mutually conflicting accepted GOOD record as `cold_episode_evidence.use='EXCLUDED', reason='CONTRADICTORY'` (and show it in read exclusions). An S2 `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT` ordinal is not accepted evidence and never becomes this analytic contradiction.

37:One `ColdSelection` has `tenant_id,site_id,code,from_at,to_at,known_at,watermark,foundation_revision_ids,binding_revision_ids,assessment_revision_id,finding_revision_ids,source_gate`. `from_at` and `to_at` are UTC and half-open; `known_at` defaults to latest committed disposition watermark captured once at request start. Omitted `from,to` selects W2-03's latest settled cold window; exactly one omitted is 422. The effective Foundation/binding is selected at each evidence source time (W2-03), with the selected policy at the requested window; conflicting/absent effective revision is an explicit limitation. 31 days maximum. No newer assessment, Finding or consequence may be silently mixed with an older `known_at`. A late accepted report makes the current projection `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until a new exact assessment and cold consequence both settle. A selected old cutoff is `HISTORICAL`, even when there is no pending work. Determine probe freshness from the latest qualifying accepted probe `source_time` within `[from,to)` compared with `to`: over 10 minutes is `STALE_SOURCE`. Later wall-clock viewing of the 2026-10-06 replay never alone makes it stale or live. Precedence: overlapping queued/failed newer work -> `PENDING_RECOMPUTE`; explicit cutoff older than latest relevant committed disposition -> `HISTORICAL`; no settled selection/probe or future `to` -> `UNKNOWN`; old last probe -> `STALE_SOURCE`; otherwise `CURRENT`. Money can remain `UNPRICED` within a current technical read; pending/old/unresolved money has its own withholding limit.
39:`ColdSiteReadService` joins, by exact UUIDs, W2-03 `ColdReadService` output, W2-01 Foundation, S4-05 source/publication links and the cold consequence head. It does not use a display-name join or the latest Finding irrespective of window. Capability claims are exactly `TEMPERATURE_POINT,EXCURSION_BOUNDS,BACKUP_START_RESULT,TRANSFER_POSITION,COOLING_RESPONSE,PRODUCT_EXPOSURE`. Each has `state:SUPPORTED|LIMITED|UNAVAILABLE|CONTRADICTORY`, `required_roles:string[]`, `missing_roles:string[]`, `coverage:{known_points:int,expected_points:int,ratio:decimal string}` and `as_of,limitation`. `TEMPERATURE_POINT` requires GOOD calibrated room probe and attested clock; `EXCURSION_BOUNDS` adds policy, two qualifying samples and >=0.8 coverage with no >10-minute bridge; `BACKUP_START_RESULT` requires correlated accepted request/result and the W2-03 causal rules; `TRANSFER_POSITION` needs its own bound transfer report; `COOLING_RESPONSE` needs an accepted compressor state and probe series; `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` means bounded thermal exposure only and requires an eligible excursion. Contradictory accepted facts give `CONTRADICTORY`, not healthy/failure. Source `contact` derives from a diagnostic received no later than selected `known_at` only when that diagnostic actually identifies the source; if only gateway-level contact exists, source contact is `UNKNOWN` with a limitation. `fact_age` is a nonnegative integer number of seconds/null between selected `to` and the last accepted source fact in `[from,to)` at the cutoff; it is distinct from contact. No source contact becomes equipment condition. Panel 3's “next evidence” is simply a named missing role/link, never a scored recommendation.
47:* `cold_consequence_revision`: `id uuid PK`, `finding_revision_id`, `episode_revision_id`, `assessment_revision_id` UUID NOT NULL; `revision_no int >0`, `prior_revision_id uuid NULL`; `kind` CHECK `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE|RECORDED_REPAIR`; `state` CHECK `UNPRICED|WITHHELD`; `reason` nonempty; `window_start,window_end timestamptz` with start<end; `room_asset_id uuid`; `overlap_group text`; `quantity_unit` CHECK `degC_min|none`; `quantity_low,quantity_high numeric NULL` nonnegative/ordered; `amount_low,amount_high numeric NULL`, `currency char(3) NULL`; `finding_basis_sha256,consequence_basis_sha256 char(64)`, `model_id` CHECK `cold-exposure/1|cold-repair/1`; `source_classification`, `source_gate_sha256 char(64)`, `assumption_revision_ids jsonb` array, `limitation text NULL`. Unique `(finding_revision_id,kind,revision_no)`, `(finding_revision_id,kind,consequence_basis_sha256)` and scoped `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` for evidence links. DB CHECK requires `amount_low IS NULL AND amount_high IS NULL AND currency IS NULL` for every row; a later approved migration must widen this. `quantity_unit='none'` requires both quantity bounds null; `degC_min` allows null bounds only when the technical claim is limited/withheld. No `RECORDED_REPAIR` row is created for the six replays because no exact accepted cost/work feed exists. `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` may carry nonnegative bounded `degC_min`; if insufficient evidence both bounds null. No zero amount or stock count stands for unknown.
52:`overlap_group = 'cold:' + sha256(canonical JSON array [tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,room_asset_id])`: literal lowercase `cold:` prefix, then 64 lowercase hex characters; it is a grouping label, not a V4 event/correlation ID (whose grammar excludes `:`). An excursion and backup Finding of the same selected room/window share it; the financial read groups `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` by this key and takes the single selected excursion exposure basis, never sums both. The start-response Finding may carry a `WITHHELD` context consequence with the same key but zero *counted* exposure entries, not a numeric zero. No cross-window summing without an explicit disjoint-window contract.
54:The source-gate event is an appended `batch_publication_link` to a previously accepted cold batch, if one exists; its trigger selects only cold heads whose linked accepted IDs use that batch and enqueues `SOURCE_GATE_CHANGED` inside the link transaction. An initial link before acceptance has no heads and creates no money work; later W2-03 Finding/episode append enqueues it. `evidence_publication` is immutable and a new unrelated publication never dirties an existing cold basis. Each trigger derives the same canonical dirty key from `(cause,locator,tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,room_asset_id)` and takes a Site advisory transaction lock before selecting heads, following `0012_evidence_consequence_work.py`'s enqueue lock pattern. The FindingRevision trigger is restricted to `NEW.domain='cold_chain'`. An episode with no warranted Finding still enqueues; the worker records a successful no-op attempt with an empty consequence ID array. No trigger on an accepted disposition bypasses W2-03 assessment: it waits for the new exact cold Finding/episode revision.
56:`cold-exposure/1` derives observed rectangle degree-minute bounds only from W2-03's persisted episode bounds/accepted evidence, not a continuous curve. A bounded positive or zero technical quantity is allowed only when W2-03 actually proved it; missing-probe/contradictory has null quantity. `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` is unpriced for sound technical bounds because no `cold_stock_quantity`, `cold_stock_unit_value`, reviewed `cold_exposure_rule`, product/currency binding or source-gated valuation exists. These are **reserved future scenario keys**, displayed as missing, not publishable via the diesel-price publisher. Product value/currency and EV tariff would be explicitly authored scenario assumptions and still would not prove sales, collections, spoilage or lost revenue. Recorded repair needs an accepted exact cost/currency/work record; none of the six replay cases has one. Mixed/unknown source, closed S4-05 gate, stale/pending assessment, incomplete period or contradictory evidence yields `WITHHELD` and null amount. LR current-money gate also refuses a current money claim from historical/pending basis. `exact_basis` returns selected Finding/episode/assessment UUIDs, accepted evidence UUIDs, source gate digest, assumption revision UUIDs (empty in W2) and consequence basis SHA. Basis SHA hashes canonical sorted IDs, window/cutoff, model/formula version, quantity bounds, source gate and missing assumption markers; an identical basis returns the existing ID and a changed basis appends a new revision.
62:`cold_site_api.py:build_cold_site_router` mounts five GET paths below `/api/sites/{site_code}/cold`. Request query for all is optional paired `from,to`, optional `known_at` (strict UTC); Analysis additionally `cursor,limit` (default 50, max 200), matching W2-03's paging spelling. Use W2-03 shared common envelope exactly: `site_id,window:{from,to,known_at},currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since},source_provenance:{classification,source_ids,evidence_ids,basis_sha256},limitations`. Every card/object includes `as_of` and `limitation`; scalar decimals serialize as decimal strings and absent values as JSON null. `currentness.state` is `CURRENT|PENDING_RECOMPUTE|STALE_SOURCE|HISTORICAL|UNKNOWN`. Valid empty selection is 200 with `UNKNOWN` and empty arrays. Use one selected repeatable-read snapshot for W2-03, source disclosure and consequence heads. No previous consequence becomes a current money claim while a relevant cold work item is queued or failed. Errors use `{error:{code,message}}`; never downgrade 503 into a blank page.

States are exactly `CURRENT|PENDING_RECOMPUTE|STALE_SOURCE|HISTORICAL|UNKNOWN`. A pending newer job returns the previous selected revision only as pending, without a current claim; an explicit unresolved revision/basis request is 409. Historical cutoff remains historical after newer evidence. Current state requires the latest relevant probe no older than 10 minutes at the read's time. Refusals use `{error:{code,message}}`: 401 auth, 403 wrong tenant or disallowed source disclosure, 404 missing/wrong-type Site or unknown scoped Finding, 409 unsettled selection/cursor basis, 422 malformed UTC/range/unknown parameter, 503 unavailable store.

.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:56:For EV session events, `accepted_evidence.asset_id` is the effective bound EV_CONNECTOR UUID and `connector_id` is its canonical public identifier; S2 resolves the equality before INSERT. Every accepted-evidence INSERT, including EVENT and EV START, retains `ON CONFLICT ON CONSTRAINT accepted_evidence_message_unique DO NOTHING`. For each ordinal, the ledger opens a savepoint around that INSERT. On a message-target `DO NOTHING` or a unique violation of `accepted_event_identity_unique` or `accepted_ev_start_unique`, it rolls back to the savepoint where needed and rereads under the same batch transaction in fixed order: message key, then event identity, then EV START identity. A matching message SHA returns `DUPLICATE`; a changed message SHA returns `MESSAGE_CONFLICT` even when the event or START key also collides. If the message key is free, a matching event SHA returns `DUPLICATE` and a changed event SHA returns `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT`; only if neither key matches does a distinct START collision return `SESSION_ID_CONFLICT`. Thus message wins a double collision, then event wins over session. Each conflict references the first accepted row for its winning key. A non-unique or unlisted unique violation propagates; never convert it to a typed conflict. Unique indexes arbitrate races; the reread is not an application precheck.

.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:115:**Time and state.** Fixed 1-minute UTC step; all updates use exact rationals/decimal text and a defined end-of-step publication order. State: chamber air temperature `T` degC, controller demand, compressor `OFF|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, grid/backup bus energized flags, generator `STOPPED|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, transfer position `GRID|BACKUP|OPEN`, door `OPEN|CLOSED`, probe `OK|FAILED`. Private forcing: ambient `Ta`, door schedule, grid loss, generator failure, probe failure; private truth never serializes into a product envelope. Initial `T=3°C`, `Ta=20°C`, door closed, compressor off, grid and bus on, generator stopped, transfer grid, probe OK. Per minute, `heat_kWh = (heat_leak_kW_per_C*(Ta-T) + door_open*door_heat_kWh*60)*1/60`; `cool_kWh = compressor_running_and_bus_powered*cooling_kW*1/60`; `T_next=T+(heat_kWh-cool_kWh)/thermal_capacity_kWh_per_C`. Defaults: thermal capacity `1.2 kWh/degC`, leak `0.08 kW/degC`, door heat `0.02 kWh/min` (stored as `1.2 kW` equivalent while open), cooling `3 kW`, compressor start delay 1 minute, generator start delay 2 minutes, transfer delay 1 minute, start timeout 5 minutes, control on `T>=4°C`, off `T<=2°C`, policy `[2,4]°C`, hysteresis 0.2°C, probe bound ±0.1°C. Clamp no state: fail run if `T` outside `[-40,60]°C`, energy residual nonzero, negative capacity/power, or impossible transition. The standalone cold pack owns its generator and bus power port; W2-02 does not compose with mini-grid.

.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:138:Excursion begins after two consecutive GOOD observations above upper band (or below lower band) at least 5 minutes apart; return requires two consecutive observations inside band by at least 0.2°C. The start is bounded by `[last in-band, first out-of-band]`; return by `[last out-of-band, first stable in-band]`; duration low = max(0, earliest return - latest start), high = latest return - earliest start, computed only across continuously covered spans. Initial out-of-band sample with no prior in-band gives unknown start; window end without return gives open episode and no finite high. `degree_minutes` bounds use observed rectangles only between adjacent usable samples <=10 minutes apart: low uses min adjacent exceedance, high max adjacent exceedance times interval minutes; add no unobserved gap area. Peak is the maximum **observed** value. An exposure Finding requires 15-minute lower duration and coverage >=0.8; otherwise `POSSIBLE` or `UNKNOWN`, never “no excursion” from missing samples. In-band fully covered is `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED` scoped to window, not equipment health.
.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:140:Start chain groups by accepted `correlation_id`, generator asset and outage window. Require ordered grid LOST → START_REQUEST → START_RESULT → generator RUNNING/FAILED → optional TRANSFER_RESULT → bus POWERED/UNPOWERED → compressor state → probe response; tolerance is source-clock bound <=30 seconds for event ordering, with timeout from effective Foundation (default 5 minutes). A failed automatic start is `OBSERVED_FAILURE` only with request, explicit failed result or timeout plus complete generator state coverage through timeout, and no contradictory success; missing request/result/state is `UNKNOWN_STEP`. A transfer-position claim requires accepted transfer event; bus energization alone proves only bus state. Cooling failure cannot be assigned from temperature alone. Emit claim levels `OBSERVED`, `BOUNDED`, `POSSIBLE`, `UNKNOWN`, `CONTRADICTORY`; explanations list exact missing/excluded records and claim ceiling.

Further amendment to `D-2026-10-18-w2-05-buildspec` (2026-10-18, Architect W2-05a QA-QD): the EV-LAB-001 Site and connector schedules use `Etc/UTC` with offset zero because the shared Site parser accepts Area/Location names; it is not widened for bare `UTC`. The five physical EV properties are charger/connector ratings, meter resolution/error bound and clock error bound; Site import limit, status/meter cadences and connector enablement are controls. An absent feed permits publication of otherwise valid declared equipment as configuration-incomplete, but scheduled service still requires a complete feedâ†’meterâ†’chargerâ†’connector path. Multiple parents always refuse; with a declared feed, unfed meters or chargers refuse. An EV Foundation has at most one `AC_BUS` feed; a second feed refuses `EV_CONFIGURATION_INVALID` so the selected single-feed read cannot silently choose one.  succeeded in 953ms:
"""Site source-health read (S7-01a, `I-source-health-read`, `D-2026-10-16-a-s7-health`).

A versioned read DTO over the central gateway store, separate from the S2
`gateway-health` contract (which stays unchanged). Two clocks are kept apart:

* **Contact** is the server receipt clock only: `CURRENT | STALE | UNKNOWN` against the
  gateway's declared cadence (`gateway_access.freshness`). A STALE contact does not date an
  outage; it says only that no receipt arrived inside two declared cadences.
* **Reported facts** are what the gateway said at its own `observed_at`: queue, disk pressure,
  clock offset and per-source device contact. Their age and clock trust are judged separately
  (`CURRENT | OLD | UNKNOWN`), so a fresh receipt of an old diagnostic is current contact with old
  facts. Queue and disk are the last *reported* state, never current hardware state.

Gateway contact never proves source-device contact: a source contact is shown only when the
selected diagnostic supplies that source's timestamp. Nothing here is a physical asset or device
health claim and nothing joins diagnostics to accepted measurement evidence.

Registration and the latest diagnostic per gateway are selected by the store in ONE repeatable-read
transaction; `check_consistent` refuses any selection whose rows disagree (409), and a store that
cannot be read is a 503, so neither can yield a current claim. There is no derived projection:
the read selects the central store directly, so `STALE_PROJECTION` is not applicable.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Protocol
from uuid import UUID

from assetops_backend.evidence.gateway_access import FRESHNESS_CADENCES, _z, freshness
from assetops_contracts.gateway_diagnostics import DiagnosticRejected, parse_canonical_gateway_diagnostics_v1_text

SCHEMA = {"id": "assetops.site-source-health", "version": 1}
RULE_ID = "assetops.source-health.two-clock.v1"
#: `D-2026-10-16-s701-clock`: two separate rules whose v1 values happen to match. Both are inclusive and
#: disclosed in the read; changing either needs a rule/DTO version review.
#: A measured clock offset with ``abs(offset_ms)`` above this makes the gateway's reported times UNTRUSTED.
CLOCK_OFFSET_LIMIT_MS = 5_000
#: An ``observed_at`` later than its server ``received_at`` by more than this is OBSERVED_AFTER_RECEIPT.
OBSERVED_AFTER_RECEIPT_ALLOWANCE_MS = 5_000
SNAPSHOT_ISOLATIONS = ("repeatable read", "serializable")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RegisteredGateway:
    tenant_id: UUID
    site_id: UUID
    gateway_id: UUID
    gateway_external_id: str
    mode: str
    registered_at: datetime
    disabled: bool
    cadence_seconds: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RegisteredSource:
    tenant_id: UUID
    site_id: UUID
    gateway_id: UUID
    source_id: UUID
    source_external_id: str
    disabled: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SelectedDiagnostic:
    tenant_id: UUID
    site_id: UUID
    gateway_id: UUID
    diagnostic_id: UUID
    sequence_epoch: str
    diagnostic_sequence: int
    observed_at: datetime
    received_at: datetime
    payload_sha256: str
    diagnostic_text: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SourceHealthSnapshot:
    """Everything one read selected, from one database snapshot."""

    tenant_id: UUID
    site_id: UUID
    isolation: str
    gateways: tuple[RegisteredGateway, ...]
    sources: tuple[RegisteredSource, ...]
    diagnostics: tuple[SelectedDiagnostic, ...]  # at most one (the latest) per gateway


class SourceHealthStore(Protocol):
    def source_health_snapshot(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID) -> SourceHealthSnapshot: ...


class SelectionInconsistent(Exception):
    """The registration and diagnostic selection disagree; no current claim is made."""

    def __init__(self, reason: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(reason)
        self.reason = reason


def _parse_instant(value: str) -> datetime:
    return datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))


def check_consistent(snapshot: SourceHealthSnapshot, read_at: datetime) -> dict[UUID, dict]:
    """Refuse a selection that is not one consistent Site snapshot; return parsed facts per gateway."""
    if snapshot.isolation.lower() not in SNAPSHOT_ISOLATIONS:
        raise SelectionInconsistent(f"selection ran at isolation {snapshot.isolation!r}, not one snapshot")
    scope = (snapshot.tenant_id, snapshot.site_id)
    gateways = {g.gateway_id: g for g in snapshot.gateways}
    if len(gateways) != len(snapshot.gateways):
        raise SelectionInconsistent("a gateway was selected twice")
    for g in snapshot.gateways:
        if (g.tenant_id, g.site_id) != scope:
            raise SelectionInconsistent(f"gateway {g.gateway_id} is not registered to this tenant Site")
    sources_by_gateway: dict[UUID, set[UUID]] = {}
    for s in snapshot.sources:
        if (s.tenant_id, s.site_id) != scope or s.gateway_id not in gateways:
            raise SelectionInconsistent(f"source {s.source_id} is not registered to a selected gateway of this Site")
        sources_by_gateway.setdefault(s.gateway_id, set()).add(s.source_id)
    parsed: dict[UUID, dict] = {}
    for d in snapshot.diagnostics:
        if (d.tenant_id, d.site_id) != scope or d.gateway_id not in gateways:
            raise SelectionInconsistent(f"diagnostic {d.diagnostic_id} is not from a selected gateway of this Site")
        if d.gateway_id in parsed:
            raise SelectionInconsistent(f"gateway {d.gateway_id} has more than one selected latest diagnostic")
        if d.received_at > read_at:
            raise SelectionInconsistent(f"diagnostic {d.diagnostic_id} was received after the read time")
        try:
            body = parse_canonical_gateway_diagnostics_v1_text(d.diagnostic_text)
        except DiagnosticRejected as error:
            raise SelectionInconsistent(f"diagnostic {d.diagnostic_id} stored text does not parse: {error}") from None
        if (body.site_id, body.gateway_id, body.sequence_epoch, body.diagnostic_sequence, body.payload_sha256) != (
                str(d.site_id), str(d.gateway_id), d.sequence_epoch, d.diagnostic_sequence, d.payload_sha256) \
                or _parse_instant(body.observed_at) != d.observed_at:
            raise SelectionInconsistent(f"diagnostic {d.diagnostic_id} row and stored text disagree")
        for source_text, _ in body.last_device_contact_by_source:
            try:
                reported_source = UUID(source_text)
            except ValueError:
                reported_source = None
            if reported_source not in sources_by_gateway.get(d.gateway_id, set()):
                raise SelectionInconsistent(
                    f"diagnostic {d.diagnostic_id} reports source {source_text}, not registered to its gateway")
        parsed[d.gateway_id] = {
            "contacts": dict(body.last_device_contact_by_source), "queue_count": body.queue_count,
            "oldest_queue_age_seconds": body.oldest_queue_age_seconds, "disk_pressure": body.disk_pressure,
            "clock_offset_ms": body.clock_offset_ms, "mapping_error_count": body.mapping_error_count,
        }
    return parsed


def _seconds(delta: timedelta) -> int:
    return int(delta.total_seconds())


def clock_trust(offset_ms: int | None) -> tuple[str, str]:
    if offset_ms is None:
        return "UNKNOWN", "OFFSET_NOT_MEASURED"
    if abs(offset_ms) > CLOCK_OFFSET_LIMIT_MS:
        return "UNTRUSTED", "OFFSET_BEYOND_TOLERANCE"
    return "WITHIN_TOLERANCE", "OFFSET_MEASURED_WITHIN_TOLERANCE"


def fact_age(observed_at: datetime | None, received_at: datetime | None, trust: str, cadence_seconds: int,
             read_at: datetime) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """`CURRENT | OLD | UNKNOWN` for the reported facts, by the gateway's own clock."""
    if observed_at is None or received_at is None:
        return "UNKNOWN", "NEVER_RECEIVED"
    if trust == "UNTRUSTED":
        return "UNKNOWN", "GATEWAY_CLOCK_UNTRUSTED"
    if observed_at - received_at > timedelta(milliseconds=OBSERVED_AFTER_RECEIPT_ALLOWANCE_MS):
        return "UNKNOWN", "OBSERVED_AFTER_RECEIPT"
    if read_at - observed_at <= timedelta(seconds=FRESHNESS_CADENCES * cadence_seconds):
        return "CURRENT", "OBSERVED_WITHIN_CADENCE_THRESHOLD"
    return "OLD", "OBSERVED_BEFORE_CADENCE_THRESHOLD"


def reported_age(at: datetime | None, trust: str, read_at: datetime) -> int | None:
    """Age of a gateway-reported time, or None when it cannot be stated: no time, an UNTRUSTED clock, or a
    time later than ``read_at`` (never a negative age; `D-2026-10-16-s701-clock` L5)."""
    if at is None or trust == "UNTRUSTED" or at > read_at:
        return None
    return _seconds(read_at - at)


def receipt_lag(received_at: datetime, observed_at: datetime) -> int | None:
    """Seconds from a gateway-reported observation to its server receipt, or None when the report says it was
    observed after it was received (an untrusted gateway clock): a negative lag is never published (W1-UX-06;
    `D-2026-10-16-s701-clock`). A legitimate nonnegative lag, including zero, is kept."""
    if observed_at > received_at:
        return None
    return _seconds(received_at - observed_at)


def _watermark(diagnostics) -> dict:
    ids = sorted(str(d.diagnostic_id) for d in diagnostics)
    latest = max((d.received_at for d in diagnostics), default=None)
    return {"selected_diagnostic_count": len(ids), "latest_received_at": _z(latest),
            "selected_diagnostic_ids_sha256": hashlib.sha256("\n".join(ids).encode("ascii")).hexdigest()}

                           access_basis: str) -> dict:
    """The S7-01a versioned Site source-health DTO. Raises `SelectionInconsistent`."""
    parsed = check_consistent(snapshot, read_at)
    latest = {d.gateway_id: d for d in snapshot.diagnostics}
    rows = []
    for g in sorted(snapshot.gateways, key=lambda g: g.gateway_external_id):
        d = latest.get(g.gateway_id)
        facts = parsed.get(g.gateway_id)
        received = None if d is None else d.received_at
        contact = freshness(received, g.cadence_seconds, read_at)
        trust, trust_reason = clock_trust(None if facts is None else facts["clock_offset_ms"])
        age_status, age_reason = fact_age(None if d is None else d.observed_at, received, trust,

**Time and state.** Fixed 1-minute UTC step; all updates use exact rationals/decimal text and a defined end-of-step publication order. State: chamber air temperature `T` degC, controller demand, compressor `OFF|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, grid/backup bus energized flags, generator `STOPPED|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, transfer position `GRID|BACKUP|OPEN`, door `OPEN|CLOSED`, probe `OK|FAILED`. Private forcing: ambient `Ta`, door schedule, grid loss, generator failure, probe failure; private truth never serializes into a product envelope. Initial `T=3Â°C`, `Ta=20Â°C`, door closed, compressor off, grid and bus on, generator stopped, transfer grid, probe OK. Per minute, `heat_kWh = (heat_leak_kW_per_C*(Ta-T) + door_open*door_heat_kWh*60)*1/60`; `cool_kWh = compressor_running_and_bus_powered*cooling_kW*1/60`; `T_next=T+(heat_kWh-cool_kWh)/thermal_capacity_kWh_per_C`. Defaults: thermal capacity `1.2 kWh/degC`, leak `0.08 kW/degC`, door heat `0.02 kWh/min` (stored as `1.2 kW` equivalent while open), cooling `3 kW`, compressor start delay 1 minute, generator start delay 2 minutes, transfer delay 1 minute, start timeout 5 minutes, control on `T>=4Â°C`, off `T<=2Â°C`, policy `[2,4]Â°C`, hysteresis 0.2Â°C, probe bound Â±0.1Â°C. Clamp no state: fail run if `T` outside `[-40,60]Â°C`, energy residual nonzero, negative capacity/power, or impossible transition. The standalone cold pack owns its generator and bus power port; W2-02 does not compose with mini-grid.

Grid loss opens transfer and deenergizes compressor bus; controller emits `START_REQUEST` only if backup enabled, generator reaches RUNNING after delay unless failed, transfer closes to BACKUP after delay if generator power exists, and compressor restarts after its own delay if controller still demands cooling. Failed start emits `START_RESULT/FAILED` at timeout; successful start emits `START_RESULT/SUCCEEDED` when running. Transfer result is emitted only if a position sensor is configured; absence stays absence. Door events are sensor transitions; door opening adds heat only while open. Probe failure emits one `UNAVAILABLE` reading with reason `SENSOR_FAILURE` if the profile publishes it, then no numeric sample; reporting-path loss emits no envelope. Device measurement is `T + fixed observation bias` (default 0), rounded to 0.1Â°C by half-even rule; no private cause field. Controller and power events use observed public transitions and stable correlation ID.

| `late-reading` | Same world forcing/initial state as `excursion`; observation hold `(19:20,PROBE_REPORT,probe-1,RELEASE_AT_20:10)`; door CLOSED. | At first cutoff 20:05, 12/13 points remain and first two high samples are 19:15/19:25; at 20:10 the accepted 19:20 point enqueues `cold_chain` recompute, changes exact evidence basis and bounds, appends a revision without rewriting the old one. |
| `contradictory` | Same world forcing/initial state as `excursion`; observation fault `(19:05,START_RESULT,generator-1,SUCCEEDED)` emits an additional distinct public controller event ID beside the genuine FAILED event, both GOOD and same correlation ID; door CLOSED. | Both events are individually valid and accepted; start-response claim is `CONTRADICTORY`, never `OBSERVED_FAILURE`. The frozen observation profile, not the physical model, creates the conflicting report. |

`cold-publication/1` emits chamber `STATE_AT_INSTANT degC` every 5 minutes including end; grid/generator/compressor/bus states on transitions and 5-minute heartbeat; compressor `INTERVAL_MEAN kW` per aligned 5-minute slot; door/start/transfer/power events on transition. `simulator/assetops_simulator/observation/transform.py` owns `cold-observation/1` hold/drop/fault transform variants, zero bias and default 3-second latency before Draft. The model retains one calibrated primary chamber probe per room; delete the two-primary-probe disagreement rule. Contradictory distinct GOOD controller outcomes for one attempt cause `CONTRADICTORY`. `cold-door-heat` property unit is `kWh/min`, with 0.02 per open minute; the equation's `*60` converts it to kW before its one-minute integration. The Lab persisted-state validator uses the `cold-thermal/1` finite vocabularies above, not the mini-grid generator lifecycle vocabulary; extend the closed identity/profile registries. `Role.value_kind` adds `EVENT`, and EVENT bindings use `conversion='IDENTITY'`, with typed outcome allowlist and no numeric conversion. Foundation controls stay NUMBER, not a new `Role.value_kind`.

The closed cold mapping uses `(device_id,signal_id)`: `(chamber-probe,temperature)` degC point, `(ambient-probe,temperature)` degC point, `(grid-meter,state)` state point, `(generator-controller,state)` state point, `(compressor-controller,state)` state point, `(backup-bus-meter,state)` state point, `(compressor-meter,power)` kW interval mean, `(door-sensor,event)` door events, `(generator-controller,start-event)` request/result events, `(transfer-controller,event)` transfer result, `(grid-meter,event)` loss/restored events, `(operator-log,temperature-event)` manual log. Each tuple is a separate versioned mapping entry with only its listed kinds, values and cadence. W2-00 also reserves EV entries `(charger-controller,session-event)` for session start/end/abort and `(connector-controller,fault-event)` for fault/clear, with connector/session identity rules above; W2-05b supplies the EV pack and its own frozen mapping version. The same signal ID on a different device is not an implicit alias (`simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py:22-46,87-107`).

Return one counted exposure per overlap group; the optional backup context row is marked nonadditive. |
--
81-| --- | --- |
82-| `cold` | 1 **What happened · power → cooling → product conditions** → `status,objective,backup_summary,causal_strip`; unknown step says which link is unobserved. 2 **Chamber temperature** → selected `/temperature.points,policy,gaps` and `temperature_summary`; gap is an empty sampled chart, not a flat line. 3 **Needs attention** → `needs_attention,findings.items`; W5 Action/Incident/Maintenance has no record. 4 **Bounded money consequence** → `consequence_summary`; `Unpriced product exposure`, null amount. 5 **Next step** → exact Finding/Evidence links or `No recorded next step`. |
83:| `cold-evidence` | 1 **What we can conclude now** → `capabilities`, coverage/claim limits. 2 **Data sources · data quality, not equipment condition** → `sources,source_provenance`, contact distinct from fact age; no condition inference. 3 **Most useful next evidence** → first missing required role and Evidence link, or `Next evidence not identified`; no ranked recommendation. |
84-| `cold-evidence-analysis` | 1 **Accepted records behind the conclusions** → paged `accepted_records` with exact ID/time/quality/exclusion/link; empty accepted set named. 2 **How a claim limit is set** → selected `claim_rules,source_provenance,limitations`; no selected assessment says `Claim limit not assessed`. |

codex
The existing rules distinguish gateway receipt from a gateway’s report about an individual source. I’ll make that distinction explicit: use the selected window end for cold source contact, keep EV Foundation’s request-time gateway check, and reserve NEVER for an explicit source-level “no contact” report.

 ## 2026-10-18
 
+Commit-3 source-contact amendment to `D-2026-10-18-w2-04-buildspec` (2026-10-18): `.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md` now fixes cold Evidence source contact against the selected window end. An explicit source contact timestamp is recent through two declared gateway cadences (inclusive), otherwise stale; an explicit null source entry is NEVER, while absent source-specific history or unverifiable timing is UNKNOWN. Gateway receipt alone does not establish source contact. W2-01 cold and W2-05a EV Foundation gateway-contact null cases remain NEVER and their gateway freshness remains request-time; W2-03 assessment and W2-04 source recency use window end. The spec gives the four plain screen labels and keeps accepted fact age separate. This is planning authority only; no implementation, review verdict or owner acceptance is implied.
+
 Commit-2 amendment to `D-2026-10-18-w2-04-buildspec` (2026-10-18): An open W2-03 excursion can have two finite **observed-segment** degree-minute bounds while its duration upper bound and end remain unknown. W2-04 therefore stores the proved consequence lower bound and a null consequence upper bound, and always carries `OPEN_EPISODE_UPPER_UNKNOWN` for an eligible open excursion; it must not present the W2-03 observed-segment high as total episode exposure. `.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md` “Commit-2 Architect rulings” confirms the implementer-proposed reason, limitation and worker error codes with exact predicates, finite allowed expansions for claim/source class, and plain screen copy. The canonical open replay needs a consequence regression assertion. This is planning authority only; it changes no implementation or review status.
 
 Decision: `D-2026-10-18-w2-06-07-buildspec`. Codex Architect/Planner publishes implementation-ready W2-06 and W2-07 packets and code-anchored specs in `tasks/W2-06-ev-assessment.md`, `tasks/W2-07-ev-site-evidence-money.md`, `.agent/W2-06-build-spec.md` and `.agent/W2-07-build-spec.md`. W2-07 follows reviewed/merged W2-06 and owns `0026_w2_ev_money`, shared Site/evidence reads, authored time-valid gross tariff scenario with exact price/source gates and five EV Site/evidence/financial routes. The specs close the implementer dry-read against current main/W2-03 and the W2-04/05 predecessor specs: fixture identities and revision publication, identifier/UTC/Decimal formats, writer and driver guards, transactional seams, refusal/currentness, six replay assertions, mock panel/row/column/chip sources or honest gaps, and per-route visual proof. Determine probe freshness from the latest qualifying accepted probe `source_time` within `[from,to)` compared with `to`: over 10 minutes is `STALE_SOURCE`. Later wall-clock viewing of the 2026-10-06 replay never alone makes it stale or live. Precedence: overlapping queued/failed newer work -> `PENDING_RECOMPUTE`; explicit cutoff older than latest relevant committed disposition -> `HISTORICAL`; no settled selection/probe or future `to` -> `UNKNOWN`; old last probe -> `STALE_SOURCE`; otherwise `CURRENT`. Money can remain `UNPRICED` within a current technical read; pending/old/unresolved money has its own withholding limit.
 
-`ColdSiteReadService` joins, by exact UUIDs, W2-03 `ColdReadService` output, W2-01 Foundation, S4-05 source/publication links and the cold consequence head. It does not use a display-name join or the latest Finding irrespective of window. Capability claims are exactly `TEMPERATURE_POINT,EXCURSION_BOUNDS,BACKUP_START_RESULT,TRANSFER_POSITION,COOLING_RESPONSE,PRODUCT_EXPOSURE`. Each has `state:SUPPORTED|LIMITED|UNAVAILABLE|CONTRADICTORY`, `required_roles:string[]`, `missing_roles:string[]`, `coverage:{known_points:int,expected_points:int,ratio:decimal string}` and `as_of,limitation`. `TEMPERATURE_POINT` requires GOOD calibrated room probe and attested clock; `EXCURSION_BOUNDS` adds policy, two qualifying samples and >=0.8 coverage with no >10-minute bridge; `BACKUP_START_RESULT` requires correlated accepted request/result and the W2-03 causal rules; `TRANSFER_POSITION` needs its own bound transfer report; `COOLING_RESPONSE` needs an accepted compressor state and probe series; `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` means bounded thermal exposure only and requires an eligible excursion. Contradictory accepted facts give `CONTRADICTORY`, not healthy/failure. Source `contact` derives from a diagnostic received no later than selected `known_at` only when that diagnostic actually identifies the source; if only gateway-level contact exists, source contact is `UNKNOWN` with a limitation. `fact_age` is a nonnegative integer number of seconds/null between selected `to` and the last accepted source fact in `[from,to)` at the cutoff; it is distinct from contact. No source contact becomes equipment condition. Panel 3's “next evidence” is simply a named missing role/link, never a scored recommendation.
+`ColdSiteReadService` joins, by exact UUIDs, W2-03 `ColdReadService` output, W2-01 Foundation, S4-05 source/publication links and the cold consequence head. It does not use a display-name join or the latest Finding irrespective of window. Capability claims are exactly `TEMPERATURE_POINT,EXCURSION_BOUNDS,BACKUP_START_RESULT,TRANSFER_POSITION,COOLING_RESPONSE,PRODUCT_EXPOSURE`. Each has `state:SUPPORTED|LIMITED|UNAVAILABLE|CONTRADICTORY`, `required_roles:string[]`, `missing_roles:string[]`, `coverage:{known_points:int,expected_points:int,ratio:decimal string}` and `as_of,limitation`. `TEMPERATURE_POINT` requires GOOD calibrated room probe and attested clock; `EXCURSION_BOUNDS` adds policy, two qualifying samples and >=0.8 coverage with no >10-minute bridge; `BACKUP_START_RESULT` requires correlated accepted request/result and the W2-03 causal rules; `TRANSFER_POSITION` needs its own bound transfer report; `COOLING_RESPONSE` needs an accepted compressor state and probe series; `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` means bounded thermal exposure only and requires an eligible excursion. Contradictory accepted facts give `CONTRADICTORY`, not healthy/failure. +**Commit-3 Architect ruling — source contact.** Select the latest registered-gateway diagnostic received no later than `known_at` in the same Site snapshot. Only its explicit `last_device_contact_by_source[source_id]` entry can establish that source's contact; gateway receipt alone cannot. A nonnull source contact instant at or before selected `to`, with a trusted gateway clock, is `RECENT` when `to - contact_at <= 2 * gateway.declared_cadence_seconds`, otherwise `STALE`. Equality is recent. This is the S7 gateway freshness multiplier, applied to the source-reported contact instant and W2-03's selected window end, not request wall time or diagnostic receipt time. An explicit null source entry is `NEVER` with null contact time: the diagnostic reports no recorded source contact. No selected diagnostic, a diagnostic without this source key, missing/unusable cadence, untrusted or unverifiable reported time, or contact time after `to` is `UNKNOWN` with a specific limitation and null claim of recency. A diagnostic read failure remains a typed unavailable read, never a successful `NEVER` or `UNKNOWN` projection. The W2-01 cold null-to-`NEVER` ruling and W2-05a EV ruling concern **gateway contact**, whose receipt freshness is checked at request time for Foundation/Readiness; they do not turn absent per-source history into `NEVER`. Cold and EV use this same gateway-versus-source distinction. On cold Evidence panel 2 use `Recent source contact`, `Last source contact is old`, `No source contact recorded`, and `Source contact unknown` respectively, with `Contact checked at window end {to}` and a separate `Last accepted fact` age. Do not call any state device health or live reporting.
 
 Analysis rows are selected accepted public records from the pinned assessment basis and exact links, ordered `(source_time,evidence_id)`, with `evidence_id,kind,source_time,received_at,quality,role,exclusion,source_id,envelope_sha256,assessment_revision_id`; `exclusion` is W2-03's SUPPORT/CONTEXT/EXCLUDED reason or null. Keep a record in the list when excluded, label why, and link to the existing Site Evidence exact record when source gate permits. `claim_rules` lists rule ID `cold-excursion/1` or `cold-start-response/1`, requirement, observed/missing role, coverage and resulting limit, all derived from the selected assessment; no speculative prose rule. Opaque cursor encodes `(source_time,evidence_id)` and selected basis SHA; mismatch returns 409 rather than changing pages.

Contradictory accepted facts give `CONTRADICTORY`, not healthy/failure. Source `contact` follows the commit-3 ruling below. `fact_age` is a nonnegative integer number of seconds/null between selected `to` and the last accepted source fact in `[from,to)` at the cutoff; it is distinct from contact. No source contact becomes equipment condition. Panel 3's “next evidence” is simply a named missing role/link, never a scored recommendation.
+
+**Commit-3 Architect ruling — source contact.** Select the latest registered-gateway diagnostic received no later than `known_at` in the same Site snapshot. Only its explicit `last_device_contact_by_source[source_id]` entry can establish that source's contact; gateway receipt alone cannot. A nonnull source contact instant at or before selected `to`, with a trusted gateway clock, is `RECENT` when `to - contact_at <= 2 * gateway.declared_cadence_seconds`, otherwise `STALE`. Equality is recent. This is the S7 gateway freshness multiplier, applied to the source-reported contact instant and W2-03's selected window end, not request wall time or diagnostic receipt time. An explicit null source entry is `NEVER` with null contact time: the diagnostic reports no recorded source contact. No selected diagnostic, a diagnostic without this source key, missing/unusable cadence, untrusted or unverifiable reported time, or contact time after `to` is `UNKNOWN` with a specific limitation and null claim of recency. A diagnostic read failure remains a typed unavailable read, never a successful `NEVER` or `UNKNOWN` projection. The W2-01 cold null-to-`NEVER` ruling and W2-05a EV ruling concern **gateway contact**, whose receipt freshness is checked at request time for Foundation/Readiness; they do not turn absent per-source history into `NEVER`. Cold and EV use this same gateway-versus-source distinction. On cold Evidence panel 2 use `Recent source contact`, `Last source contact is old`, `No source contact recorded`, and `Source contact unknown` respectively, with `Contact checked at window end {to}` and a separate `Last accepted fact` age. Do not call any state device health or live reporting.
 
 Analysis rows are selected accepted public records from the pinned assessment basis and exact links, ordered `(source_time,evidence_id)`, with `evidence_id,kind,source_time,received_at,quality,role,exclusion,source_id,envelope_sha256,assessment_revision_id`; `exclusion` is W2-03's SUPPORT/CONTEXT/EXCLUDED reason or null. Keep a record in the list when excluded, label why, and link to the existing Site Evidence exact record when source gate permits. `claim_rules` lists rule ID `cold-excursion/1` or `cold-start-response/1`, requirement, observed/missing role, coverage and resulting limit, all derived from the selected assessment; no speculative prose rule. Opaque cursor encodes `(source_time,evidence_id)` and selected basis SHA; mismatch returns 409 rather than changing pages.

W2-04 therefore stores the proved consequence lower bound and a null consequence upper bound, and always carries `OPEN_EPISODE_UPPER_UNKNOWN` for an eligible open excursion; it must not present the W2-03 observed-segment high as total episode exposure. `.agent/W2-04-build-spec.md` “Commit-2 Architect rulings” confirms the implementer-proposed reason, limitation and worker error codes with exact predicates, finite allowed expansions for claim/source class, and plain screen copy. +**Commit-3 Architect ruling — source contact.** Select the latest registered-gateway diagnostic received no later than `known_at` in the same Site snapshot. Only its explicit `last_device_contact_by_source[source_id]` entry can establish that source's contact; gateway receipt alone cannot. Equality is recent. This is the S7 gateway freshness multiplier, applied to the source-reported contact instant and W2-03's selected window end, not request wall time or diagnostic receipt time. An explicit null source entry is `NEVER` with null contact time: the diagnostic reports no recorded source contact. A diagnostic read failure remains a typed unavailable read, never a successful `NEVER` or `UNKNOWN` projection. The W2-01 cold null-to-`NEVER` ruling and W2-05a EV ruling concern **gateway contact**, whose receipt freshness is checked at request time for Foundation/Readiness; they do not turn absent per-source history into `NEVER`. Cold and EV use this same gateway-versus-source distinction. On cold Evidence panel 2 use `Recent source contact`, `Last source contact is old`, `No source contact recorded`, and `Source contact unknown` respectively, with `Contact checked at window end {to}` and a separate `Last accepted fact` age.
